```rust
 95 | /// Analyze whitespace, comments, and unicode content.
 96 | pub fn analyze_content(text: &[u8]) -> ContentStats {
 97 |     let mut stats = ContentStats::default();
 98 |     stats.total_bytes = text.len() as u64;
 99 | 
100 |     let len = text.len();
101 |     let mut i = 0;
102 |     let mut line_start = 0;
103 |     let mut in_line_start = true; // tracking indent at line start
104 |     let mut line_has_spaces = false;
105 |     let mut line_has_tabs = false;
106 | 
107 |     while i < len {
108 |         let b = text[i];
109 | 
110 |         // Newline: analyze the line
111 |         if b == b'\n' {
112 |             stats.total_lines += 1;
113 |             let line = &text[line_start..i];
114 |             if line.iter().all(|&b| b == b' ' || b == b'\t' || b == b'\r') {
115 |                 stats.blank_lines += 1;
116 |             }
117 |             // Check indent style
118 |             if line_has_spaces && line_has_tabs {
119 |                 stats.mixed_indent += 1;
120 |             }
121 |             // Detect indent level from leading spaces
122 |             let leading_spaces = line.iter().take_while(|&&b| b == b' ').count();
123 |             if leading_spaces > 0 && leading_spaces % 8 == 0 {
124 |                 stats.indent_8 += 1;
125 |             } else if leading_spaces > 0 && leading_spaces % 4 == 0 {
126 |                 stats.indent_4 += 1;
127 |             } else if leading_spaces > 0 && leading_spaces % 2 == 0 {
128 |                 stats.indent_2 += 1;
129 |             }
130 |             if line.first() == Some(&b'\t') {
131 |                 stats.tab_indent += 1;
132 |             }
133 |             line_start = i + 1;
134 |             in_line_start = true;
135 |             line_has_spaces = false;
136 |             line_has_tabs = false;
137 |             i += 1;
138 |             continue;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         // Track indent characters at line start
142 |         if in_line_start {
143 |             if b == b' ' {
144 |                 line_has_spaces = true;
145 |                 i += 1;
146 |                 continue;
147 |             } else if b == b'\t' {
148 |                 line_has_tabs = true;
149 |                 i += 1;
150 |                 continue;
151 |             } else {
152 |                 in_line_start = false;
153 |             }
154 |         }
155 | 
156 |         // Count space runs
157 |         if b == b' ' {
158 |             let start = i;
159 |             while i < len && text[i] == b' ' {
160 |                 i += 1;
161 |             }
162 |             let run = i - start;
163 |             if run == 1 {
164 |                 stats.single_spaces += 1;
165 |             } else {
166 |                 stats.multi_spaces += 1;
167 |             }
168 |             continue;
169 |         }
170 | 
171 |         // Count tab runs
172 |         if b == b'\t' {
173 |             let start = i;
174 |             while i < len && text[i] == b'\t' {
175 |                 i += 1;
176 |             }
177 |             let run = i - start;
178 |             if run == 1 {
179 |                 stats.single_tabs += 1;
180 |             } else {
181 |                 stats.multi_tabs += 1;
182 |             }
183 |             continue;
184 |         }
185 | 
186 |         // Line comments
187 |         if b == b'/' && i + 1 < len && text[i + 1] == b'/' {
188 |             stats.line_comments += 1;
189 |             let comment_start = i;
190 |             i += 2;
191 |             let content_start = i;
192 |             while i < len && text[i] != b'\n' {
193 |                 i += 1;
194 |             }
195 |             let comment_len = i - comment_start;
196 |             stats.comment_bytes += comment_len as u64;
197 |             // Check for non-ASCII in comment
198 |             let comment_content = &text[content_start..i];
199 |             let non_ascii_in_comment = comment_content.iter().filter(|&&b| b > 127).count() as u64;
200 |             if non_ascii_in_comment > 0 {
201 |                 stats.non_ascii_bytes += non_ascii_in_comment;
202 |                 stats.non_ascii_comments += 1;
203 |                 stats.has_non_ascii_comment = true;
204 |                 // Extract sample (first non-English comment, max 3)
205 |                 if stats.non_english_samples.len() < 3 {
206 |                     if let Ok(s) = std::str::from_utf8(comment_content) {
207 |                         let trimmed = s.trim();
208 |                         if !trimmed.is_empty() && trimmed.len() < 200 {
209 |                             stats.non_english_samples.push(trimmed.to_owned());
210 |                         }
211 |                     }
212 |                 }
213 |             }
214 |             continue;
215 |         }
216 | 
217 |         // Block comments
218 |         if b == b'/' && i + 1 < len && text[i + 1] == b'*' {
219 |             stats.block_comments += 1;
220 |             let comment_start = i;
221 |             i += 2;
222 |             let content_start = i;
223 |             while i + 1 < len && !(text[i] == b'*' && text[i + 1] == b'/') {
224 |                 i += 1;
225 |             }
226 |             let comment_content = &text[content_start..i];
227 |             if i + 1 < len {
228 |                 i += 2;
229 |             }
230 |             stats.comment_bytes += (i - comment_start) as u64;
231 |             let non_ascii_in_block = comment_content.iter().filter(|&&b| b > 127).count() as u64;
232 |             if non_ascii_in_block > 0 {
233 |                 stats.non_ascii_bytes += non_ascii_in_block;
234 |                 stats.non_ascii_comments += 1;
235 |                 stats.has_non_ascii_comment = true;
236 |                 if stats.non_english_samples.len() < 3 {
237 |                     if let Ok(s) = std::str::from_utf8(comment_content) {
238 |                         let trimmed = s.trim();
239 |                         if !trimmed.is_empty() && trimmed.len() < 200 {
240 |                             stats.non_english_samples.push(trimmed.to_owned());
241 |                         }
242 |                     }
243 |                 }
244 |             }
245 |             continue;
246 |         }
247 | 
248 |         // String literals
249 |         if b == b'"' {
250 |             let start = i;
251 |             i += 1;
252 |             while i < len && text[i] != b'"' {
253 |                 if text[i] == b'\\' {
254 |                     i += 1;
255 |                 }
256 |                 i += 1;
257 |             }
258 |             if i < len {
259 |                 i += 1;
260 |             }
261 |             stats.string_bytes += (i - start) as u64;
262 |             continue;
263 |         }
264 | 
265 |         // Non-ASCII byte detection
266 |         if b > 127 {
267 |             stats.non_ascii_bytes += 1;
268 |             // Count UTF-8 multi-byte sequences
269 |             if b & 0xE0 == 0xC0 {
270 |                 stats.utf8_chars += 1;
271 |                 i += 2;
272 |             } else if b & 0xF0 == 0xE0 {
273 |                 stats.utf8_chars += 1;
274 |                 i += 3;
275 |             } else if b & 0xF8 == 0xF0 {
276 |                 stats.utf8_chars += 1;
277 |                 i += 4;
278 |             } else {
279 |                 i += 1;
280 |             }
281 |             continue;
282 |         }
283 | 
284 |         i += 1;
285 |     }
286 | 
287 |     // Count last line if doesn't end with newline
288 |     if line_start < len {
289 |         stats.total_lines += 1;
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     stats
293 | }
294 | 
295 | #[derive(Default, Clone)]
296 | pub struct StyleCounts {
297 |     pub snake_case: u64,
298 |     pub camel_case: u64,
299 |     pub pascal_case: u64,
300 |     pub screaming_case: u64,
301 |     pub mixed_other: u64,
302 | }
303 | 
304 | impl StyleCounts {
305 |     pub fn merge(&mut self, other: &StyleCounts) {
306 |         self.snake_case += other.snake_case;
307 |         self.camel_case += other.camel_case;
308 |         self.pascal_case += other.pascal_case;
309 |         self.screaming_case += other.screaming_case;
310 |         self.mixed_other += other.mixed_other;
311 |     }
312 | }
313 | 
314 | /// Classify an identifier's naming style.
315 | fn classify_style(ident: &str) -> &'static str {
316 |     // Strip leading/trailing underscores for classification (e.g., __func__ -> func)
317 |     let stripped = ident.trim_matches('_');
318 |     if stripped.is_empty() {
319 |         return "other";
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     let has_underscore = stripped.contains('_');
323 |     let has_lower = stripped.bytes().any(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase());
324 |     let has_upper = stripped.bytes().any(|b| b.is_ascii_uppercase());
325 | 
326 |     // Dunder patterns like __func__, __attribute__ → "other"
327 |     if ident.starts_with("__") || ident.ends_with("__") {
328 |         return "other";
329 |     }
330 | 
331 |     if !has_lower && has_upper && stripped.len() > 1 {
332 |         return "screaming"; // SCREAMING_CASE or ALLCAPS
333 |     }
334 |     if has_underscore && has_lower && !has_upper {
335 |         return "snake"; // snake_case
336 |     }
337 |     if !has_underscore && has_lower && has_upper {
338 |         if stripped.as_bytes()[0].is_ascii_uppercase() {
339 |             return "pascal"; // PascalCase
340 |         }
341 |         return "camel"; // camelCase
342 |     }
343 |     "other"
344 | }
345 | 
346 | /// Extract the namespace prefix from a qualified identifier like "std::vector".
347 | fn extract_namespace(ident: &str) -> Option<&str> {
348 |     if let Some(pos) = ident.find("::") {
349 |         let ns = &ident[..pos];
350 |         if !ns.is_empty() && ns.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'_') {
351 |             return Some(ns);
352 |         }
353 |     }
354 |     None
355 | }
356 | 
357 | /// Split a C++ identifier into morpheme components.
358 | /// Handles snake_case, camelCase, PascalCase, and SCREAMING_CASE.
359 | pub fn split_identifier(ident: &str) -> Vec<String> {
360 |     let mut parts = Vec::new();
361 |     for chunk in ident.split('_') {
362 |         if chunk.is_empty() {
363 |             continue;
364 |         }
365 |         // Split camelCase: maxActiveBlocks -> [max, Active, Blocks]
366 |         let bytes = chunk.as_bytes();
367 |         let mut start = 0;
368 |         let mut i = 1;
369 |         while i < bytes.len() {
370 |             let curr_upper = bytes[i].is_ascii_uppercase();
371 |             let prev_upper = bytes[i - 1].is_ascii_uppercase();
372 | 
373 |             if curr_upper && !prev_upper {
374 |                 // Transition: lower->UPPER (camelCase boundary)
375 |                 let part = &chunk[start..i];
376 |                 if part.len() > 1 {
377 |                     parts.push(part.to_ascii_lowercase());
378 |                 }
379 |                 start = i;
380 |             } else if !curr_upper && prev_upper && i - start > 1 {
381 |                 // Transition: UPPER->lower after UPPER run (e.g., HTTPSConn -> HTTPS, Conn)
382 |                 let part = &chunk[start..i - 1];
383 |                 if part.len() > 1 {
384 |                     parts.push(part.to_ascii_lowercase());
385 |                 }
386 |                 start = i - 1;
387 |             }
388 |             i += 1;
389 |         }
390 |         // Last part
391 |         let part = &chunk[start..];
392 |         if part.len() > 1 {
393 |             parts.push(part.to_ascii_lowercase());
394 |         }
395 |     }
396 |     parts
397 | }
398 | 
399 | /// Analyze a single C++ document. Returns all extracted counts.
400 | ///
401 | /// Uses a hand-written scanner (no regex) for maximum throughput.
402 | /// Single pass through the document extracts identifiers and $-operators.
403 | /// String literals are extracted separately for SQL keyword detection.
404 | pub fn analyze_document(text: &[u8], do_morphemes: bool, do_content: bool) -> DocResult {
405 |     let mut ident_counts: HashMap<String, u64> = HashMap::with_capacity(4096);
406 |     let mut dollar_counts: HashMap<String, u64> = HashMap::with_capacity(64);
407 |     let mut sql_keyword_counts: HashMap<String, u64> = HashMap::with_capacity(64);
408 |     let mut morpheme_counts: HashMap<String, u64> = HashMap::with_capacity(1024);
409 |     let mut style_counts = StyleCounts::default();
410 |     let mut namespace_counts: HashMap<String, u64> = HashMap::with_capacity(64);
411 |     let mut total_idents: u64 = 0;
412 |     let mut total_ident_len: u64 = 0;
413 | 
414 |     let len = text.len();
415 |     let mut i = 0;
416 | 
417 |     while i < len {
418 |         let b = text[i];
419 | 
420 |         // Skip line comments
421 |         if b == b'/' && i + 1 < len {
422 |             if text[i + 1] == b'/' {
423 |                 // Line comment: skip to end of line
424 |                 i += 2;
425 |                 while i < len && text[i] != b'\n' {
426 |                     i += 1;
427 |                 }
428 |                 continue;
429 |             }
430 |             if text[i + 1] == b'*' {
431 |                 // Block comment: skip to */
432 |                 i += 2;
433 |                 while i + 1 < len && !(text[i] == b'*' && text[i + 1] == b'/') {
434 |                     i += 1;
435 |                 }
436 |                 i += 2; // skip */
437 |                 continue;
438 |             }
439 |         }
440 | 
441 |         // String literal: extract content for SQL keyword analysis
442 |         if b == b'"' {
443 |             i += 1;
444 |             let str_start = i;
445 |             while i < len && text[i] != b'"' {
446 |                 if text[i] == b'\\' {
447 |                     i += 1; // skip escaped char
448 |                 }
449 |                 i += 1;
450 |             }
451 |             // Extract SQL keywords from string content
452 |             let str_content = &text[str_start..i.min(len)];
453 |             extract_sql_keywords(str_content, &mut sql_keyword_counts);
454 |             if i < len {
455 |                 i += 1; // skip closing quote
456 |             }
457 |             continue;
458 |         }
459 | 
460 |         // Character literal: skip
461 |         if b == b'\'' {
462 |             i += 1;
463 |             if i < len && text[i] == b'\\' {
464 |                 i += 1;
465 |             }
466 |             if i < len {
467 |                 i += 1; // skip char
468 |             }
469 |             if i < len && text[i] == b'\'' {
470 |                 i += 1;
471 |             }
472 |             continue;
473 |         }
474 | 
475 |         // $-prefixed operator (MongoDB)
476 |         if b == b'$' && i + 1 < len && is_ident_start(text[i + 1]) {
477 |             let start = i;
478 |             i += 1;
479 |             while i < len && is_ident_continue(text[i]) {
480 |                 i += 1;
481 |             }
482 |             if let Ok(s) = std::str::from_utf8(&text[start..i]) {
483 |                 *dollar_counts.entry(s.to_owned()).or_default() += 1;
484 |             }
485 |             continue;
486 |         }
487 | 
488 |         // Identifier (possibly namespace-qualified with ::)
489 |         if is_ident_start(b) {
490 |             let start = i;
491 |             i += 1;
492 |             while i < len && is_ident_continue(text[i]) {
493 |                 i += 1;
494 |             }
495 |             // Check for :: (namespace qualification)
496 |             while i + 1 < len && text[i] == b':' && text[i + 1] == b':' {
497 |                 i += 2; // skip ::
498 |                 while i < len && is_ident_continue(text[i]) {
499 |                     i += 1;
500 |                 }
501 |             }
502 | 
503 |             if let Ok(ident) = std::str::from_utf8(&text[start..i]) {
504 |                 total_idents += 1;
505 |                 total_ident_len += ident.len() as u64;
506 | 
507 |                 // Count the full identifier (including namespace)
508 |                 *ident_counts.entry(ident.to_owned()).or_default() += 1;
509 | 
510 |                 // Also count the last component separately for non-qualified lookups
511 |                 if ident.contains("::") {
512 |                     if let Some(last) = ident.rsplit("::").next() {
513 |                         if !last.is_empty() {
514 |                             *ident_counts.entry(last.to_owned()).or_default() += 1;
515 |                         }
516 |                     }
517 |                     // Track namespace
518 |                     if let Some(ns) = extract_namespace(ident) {
519 |                         *namespace_counts.entry(ns.to_owned()).or_default() += 1;
520 |                     }
521 |                 }
522 | 
523 |                 // Classify naming style (only for non-trivial identifiers)
524 |                 let base = if let Some(last) = ident.rsplit("::").next() {
525 |                     last
526 |                 } else {
527 |                     ident
528 |                 };
529 |                 if base.len() > 2 {
530 |                     match classify_style(base) {
531 |                         "snake" => style_counts.snake_case += 1,
532 |                         "camel" => style_counts.camel_case += 1,
533 |                         "pascal" => style_counts.pascal_case += 1,
534 |                         "screaming" => style_counts.screaming_case += 1,
535 |                         _ => style_counts.mixed_other += 1,
536 |                     }
537 |                 }
538 | 
539 |                 // Morpheme analysis
540 |                 if do_morphemes && base.len() > 3 {
541 |                     let freq = 1u64; // count per occurrence, not per unique
542 |                     for part in split_identifier(base) {
543 |                         *morpheme_counts.entry(part).or_default() += freq;
544 |                     }
545 |                 }
546 |             }
547 |             continue;
548 |         }
549 | 
550 |         // Skip preprocessor lines starting with #
551 |         if b == b'#' {
552 |             // Skip to end of line (including \ continuations)
553 |             while i < len {
554 |                 if text[i] == b'\n' {
555 |                     if i > 0 && text[i - 1] == b'\\' {
556 |                         i += 1; // line continuation
557 |                         continue;
558 |                     }
559 |                     break;
560 |                 }
561 |                 i += 1;
562 |             }
563 |             continue;
564 |         }
565 | 
566 |         i += 1;
567 |     }
568 | 
569 |     let unique_idents = ident_counts.len() as u64;
570 | 
571 |     // Content analysis (whitespace, comments, unicode) - only when requested
572 |     let content_stats = if do_content { analyze_content(text) } else { ContentStats::default() };
573 | 
574 |     DocResult {
575 |         ident_counts,
576 |         dollar_counts,
577 |         sql_keyword_counts,
578 |         morpheme_counts,
579 |         style_counts,
580 |         namespace_counts,
581 |         total_idents,
582 |         unique_idents,
583 |         total_ident_len,
584 |         content_stats,
585 |     }
586 | }
587 | 
588 | /// Extract uppercase SQL keywords from a string literal's content.
589 | fn extract_sql_keywords(content: &[u8], counts: &mut HashMap<String, u64>) {
590 |     let len = content.len();
591 |     let mut i = 0;
592 |     while i < len {
593 |         // Find start of uppercase word
594 |         if content[i].is_ascii_uppercase() {
595 |             let start = i;
596 |             i += 1;
597 |             while i < len && (content[i].is_ascii_uppercase() || content[i] == b'_') {
598 |                 i += 1;
599 |             }
600 |             let word_len = i - start;
601 |             if word_len >= 2 {
602 |                 if let Ok(word) = std::str::from_utf8(&content[start..i]) {
603 |                     *counts.entry(word.to_owned()).or_default() += 1;
604 |                 }
605 |             }
606 |         } else {
607 |             i += 1;
608 |         }
609 |     }
610 | }
611 | 
612 | #[inline(always)]
613 | fn is_ident_start(b: u8) -> bool {
614 |     b.is_ascii_alphabetic() || b == b'_'
615 | }
616 | 
617 | #[inline(always)]
618 | fn is_ident_continue(b: u8) -> bool {
619 |     b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'_'
620 | }
621 | 
622 | #[cfg(test)]
623 | mod tests {
624 |     use super::*;
625 | 
626 |     #[test]
627 |     fn test_split_identifier() {
628 |         assert_eq!(split_identifier("snake_case_name"), vec!["snake", "case", "name"]);
629 |         assert_eq!(split_identifier("camelCaseName"), vec!["camel", "case", "name"]);
630 |         assert_eq!(split_identifier("PascalCaseName"), vec!["pascal", "case", "name"]);
631 |         assert_eq!(split_identifier("HTTPSConnection"), vec!["https", "connection"]);
632 |         assert_eq!(split_identifier("getHTTPSUrl"), vec!["get", "https", "url"]);
633 |         assert_eq!(split_identifier("a"), Vec::<String>::new()); // too short
634 |     }
635 | 
636 |     #[test]
637 |     fn test_classify_style() {
638 |         assert_eq!(classify_style("snake_case"), "snake");
639 |         assert_eq!(classify_style("camelCase"), "camel");
640 |         assert_eq!(classify_style("PascalCase"), "pascal");
641 |         assert_eq!(classify_style("SCREAMING_CASE"), "screaming");
642 |         assert_eq!(classify_style("__func__"), "other");
643 |     }
644 | 
645 |     #[test]
646 |     fn test_analyze_cuda() {
647 |         let src = b"__device__ void kernel() { int idx = threadIdx.x + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x; }";
648 |         let result = analyze_document(src, false, false);
649 |         assert!(result.ident_counts.contains_key("__device__"));
650 |         assert!(result.ident_counts.contains_key("threadIdx"));
651 |         assert!(result.ident_counts.contains_key("blockIdx"));
652 |     }
653 | 
654 |     #[test]
655 |     fn test_analyze_sql_in_string() {
656 |         let src = br#"const char* q = "SELECT name FROM users WHERE id = 1";"#;
657 |         let result = analyze_document(src, false, false);
658 |         assert!(result.sql_keyword_counts.contains_key("SELECT"));
659 |         assert!(result.sql_keyword_counts.contains_key("FROM"));
660 |         assert!(result.sql_keyword_counts.contains_key("WHERE"));
661 |     }
662 | 
663 |     #[test]
664 |     fn test_dollar_ops() {
665 |         let src = br#"auto pipeline = bsoncxx::builder::stream::document{} << "$match" << "$group";"#;
666 |         let result = analyze_document(src, false, false);
667 |         // Dollar ops inside strings won't be captured by the $ scanner
668 |         // They need to appear as identifiers like: $match
669 |         let src2 = b"$match $group $sort";
670 |         let result2 = analyze_document(src2, false, false);
671 |         assert!(result2.dollar_counts.contains_key("$match"));
672 |         assert!(result2.dollar_counts.contains_key("$group"));
673 |         let _ = result; // use it
674 |     }
675 | 
676 |     #[test]
677 |     fn test_namespace_extraction() {
678 |         let src = b"std::vector<int> v; boost::asio::ip::tcp::endpoint ep;";
679 |         let result = analyze_document(src, false, false);
680 |         assert!(result.namespace_counts.contains_key("std"));
681 |         assert!(result.namespace_counts.contains_key("boost"));
682 |     }
683 | 
684 |     #[test]
685 |     fn test_morpheme_analysis() {
686 |         let src = b"void initializeBuffer() { createHandler(); destroyManager(); }";
687 |         let result = analyze_document(src, true, false);
688 |         assert!(*result.morpheme_counts.get("initialize").unwrap_or(&0) > 0
689 |             || *result.morpheme_counts.get("init").unwrap_or(&0) > 0);
690 |         assert!(*result.morpheme_counts.get("buffer").unwrap_or(&0) > 0);
691 |     }
692 | 
693 |     #[test]
694 |     fn test_skips_comments() {
695 |         let src = b"// cudaMalloc is in a comment\nint x; /* cudaFree also comment */\ncudaMemcpy();";
696 |         let result = analyze_document(src, false, false);
697 |         assert!(!result.ident_counts.contains_key("cudaMalloc"));
698 |         assert!(!result.ident_counts.contains_key("cudaFree"));
699 |         assert!(result.ident_counts.contains_key("cudaMemcpy"));
700 |     }
701 | 
702 |     #[test]
703 |     fn test_content_analysis() {
704 |         let src = b"// English comment\nint x = 0;\n    int y; // \xc3\xa9tranger non-ASCII\n/* block */\n\n\"hello world\";\n\t\tint z;\n";
705 |         let stats = analyze_content(src);
706 |         assert!(stats.total_lines >= 6);
707 |         assert_eq!(stats.line_comments, 2);
708 |         assert_eq!(stats.block_comments, 1);
709 |         assert!(stats.blank_lines >= 1);
710 |         assert!(stats.non_ascii_bytes > 0);
711 |         assert!(stats.has_non_ascii_comment);
712 |         assert!(stats.string_bytes > 0);
713 |         assert!(stats.tab_indent >= 1);
714 |     }
715 | }
```

```rust
   1 | /// vocab-analyzer: High-performance C++ vocabulary frequency analyzer.
   2 | ///
   3 | /// Scans C++ source files (raw directories, JSONL, or individual files) to validate
   4 | /// proposed tokenizer vocabulary against actual usage. Reports per-category coverage,
   5 | /// per-token frequency, morpheme analysis, naming style distribution, and namespace usage.
   6 | ///
   7 | /// Optimized for large corpora (200GB+) with:
   8 | /// - Hand-written O(n) identifier scanner (no regex in hot path)

  87 | /// Aggregated results across all documents.
  88 | struct GlobalResult {
  89 |     /// Per-token counts (ident + namespace-stripped)
  90 |     ident_counts: HashMap<String, u64>,
  91 |     /// Per-token document frequency
  92 |     doc_presence: HashMap<String, u64>,
  93 |     /// Dollar operator counts
  94 |     dollar_counts: HashMap<String, u64>,
  95 |     dollar_doc_presence: HashMap<String, u64>,
  96 |     /// SQL keyword counts (in string literals)
  97 |     sql_keyword_counts: HashMap<String, u64>,

  99 |     /// Morpheme component counts
 100 |     morpheme_counts: HashMap<String, u64>,

 102 |     style_counts: StyleCounts,
 103 |     /// Namespace usage
 104 |     namespace_counts: HashMap<String, u64>,
 105 |     /// C++ keyword frequency
 106 |     keyword_counts: HashMap<String, u64>,
 107 |     /// Content stats (whitespace, comments, unicode)

 120 | impl GlobalResult {
 121 |     fn new() -> Self {
 122 |         Self {
 123 |             ident_counts: HashMap::new(),
 124 |             doc_presence: HashMap::new(),
 125 |             dollar_counts: HashMap::new(),
 126 |             dollar_doc_presence: HashMap::new(),
 127 |             sql_keyword_counts: HashMap::new(),
 128 |             sql_doc_presence: HashMap::new(),
 129 |             morpheme_counts: HashMap::new(),
 130 |             style_counts: StyleCounts::default(),
 131 |             namespace_counts: HashMap::new(),
 132 |             keyword_counts: HashMap::new(),
 133 |             content_stats: ContentStats::default(),
 134 |             docs_with_non_ascii_comments: 0,
 135 |             non_english_samples: Vec::new(),
 136 |             num_docs: 0,
 137 |             total_idents: 0,
 138 |             total_ident_len: 0,
 139 |             total_unique_idents: 0,
 140 |         }

 143 |     fn merge_doc(&mut self, doc: DocResult) {
 144 |         self.num_docs += 1;
 145 |         self.total_idents += doc.total_idents;
 146 |         self.total_ident_len += doc.total_ident_len;
 147 |         self.total_unique_idents += doc.unique_idents;
 148 |         self.style_counts.merge(&doc.style_counts);
 149 |         self.content_stats.merge(&doc.content_stats);
 150 |         if doc.content_stats.has_non_ascii_comment {
 151 |             self.docs_with_non_ascii_comments += 1;
 152 |             if self.non_english_samples.len() < 50 {
 153 |                 for sample in &doc.content_stats.non_english_samples {
 154 |                     if self.non_english_samples.len() < 50 {
 155 |                         self.non_english_samples.push(sample.clone());
 156 |                     }
 157 |                 }
 158 |             }
 159 |         }
 160 | 
 161 |         // Merge ident counts + doc presence
 162 |         for (k, v) in &doc.ident_counts {
 163 |             *self.ident_counts.entry(k.clone()).or_default() += v;
 164 |         }
 165 |         for k in doc.ident_counts.keys() {
 166 |             *self.doc_presence.entry(k.clone()).or_default() += 1;
 167 |         }
 168 | 
 169 |         // Dollar
 170 |         for (k, v) in &doc.dollar_counts {
 171 |             *self.dollar_counts.entry(k.clone()).or_default() += v;
 172 |         }
 173 |         for k in doc.dollar_counts.keys() {
 174 |             *self.dollar_doc_presence.entry(k.clone()).or_default() += 1;
 175 |         }
 176 | 
 177 |         // SQL
 178 |         for (k, v) in &doc.sql_keyword_counts {
 179 |             *self.sql_keyword_counts.entry(k.clone()).or_default() += v;
 180 |         }
 181 |         for k in doc.sql_keyword_counts.keys() {
 182 |             *self.sql_doc_presence.entry(k.clone()).or_default() += 1;
 183 |         }
 184 | 
 185 |         // Morphemes
 186 |         for (k, v) in &doc.morpheme_counts {
 187 |             *self.morpheme_counts.entry(k.clone()).or_default() += v;
 188 |         }
 189 | 
 190 |         // Namespaces
 191 |         for (k, v) in &doc.namespace_counts {
 192 |             *self.namespace_counts.entry(k.clone()).or_default() += v;
 193 |         }
 194 |     }
 195 | 
 196 |     /// Compute C++ keyword frequencies from ident_counts.
 197 |     fn compute_keyword_counts(&mut self) {
 198 |         for &kw in CPP_KEYWORDS {
 199 |             if let Some(&count) = self.ident_counts.get(kw) {
 200 |                 self.keyword_counts.insert(kw.to_owned(), count);
 201 |             }
 202 |         }
 203 |     }
 204 | }

 206 | /// Merge two GlobalResults (for combining per-thread results).
 207 | fn merge_globals(a: &mut GlobalResult, b: GlobalResult) {
 208 |     a.num_docs += b.num_docs;
 209 |     a.total_idents += b.total_idents;
 210 |     a.total_ident_len += b.total_ident_len;
 211 |     a.total_unique_idents += b.total_unique_idents;
 212 |     a.style_counts.merge(&b.style_counts);
 213 |     a.content_stats.merge(&b.content_stats);
 214 |     a.docs_with_non_ascii_comments += b.docs_with_non_ascii_comments;
 215 |     for sample in b.non_english_samples {
 216 |         if a.non_english_samples.len() < 50 {
 217 |             a.non_english_samples.push(sample);
 218 |         }
 219 |     }
 220 | 
 221 |     for (k, v) in b.ident_counts {
 222 |         *a.ident_counts.entry(k).or_default() += v;
 223 |     }
 224 |     for (k, v) in b.doc_presence {
 225 |         *a.doc_presence.entry(k).or_default() += v;
 226 |     }
 227 |     for (k, v) in b.dollar_counts {
 228 |         *a.dollar_counts.entry(k).or_default() += v;
 229 |     }
 230 |     for (k, v) in b.dollar_doc_presence {
 231 |         *a.dollar_doc_presence.entry(k).or_default() += v;
 232 |     }
 233 |     for (k, v) in b.sql_keyword_counts {
 234 |         *a.sql_keyword_counts.entry(k).or_default() += v;
 235 |     }
 236 |     for (k, v) in b.sql_doc_presence {
 237 |         *a.sql_doc_presence.entry(k).or_default() += v;
 238 |     }
 239 |     for (k, v) in b.morpheme_counts {
 240 |         *a.morpheme_counts.entry(k).or_default() += v;
 241 |     }
 242 |     for (k, v) in b.namespace_counts {
 243 |         *a.namespace_counts.entry(k).or_default() += v;
 244 |     }

 247 | // ─── File Collection ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 248 | 
 249 | fn collect_files(inputs: &[String], extensions: &str) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
 250 |     let exts: Vec<&str> = extensions.split(',').collect();
 251 |     let mut files = Vec::new();
 252 | 
 253 |     for input in inputs {
 254 |         let path = PathBuf::from(input);
 255 | 
 256 |         if path.is_dir() {
 257 |             // Walk directory recursively
 258 |             for entry in WalkDir::new(&path)
 259 |                 .follow_links(true)
 260 |                 .into_iter()
 261 |                 .filter_map(|e| e.ok())
 262 |             {
 263 |                 if entry.file_type().is_file() {
 264 |                     if let Some(ext) = entry.path().extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) {
 265 |                         if exts.iter().any(|&e2| e2.eq_ignore_ascii_case(ext)) {
 266 |                             files.push(entry.into_path());
 267 |                         }
 268 |                     }
 269 |                 }
 270 |             }
 271 |         } else if path.extension().map_or(false, |e| e == "jsonl" || e == "json") {
 272 |             // JSONL file: read later
 273 |             files.push(path);
 274 |         } else if path.is_file() {
 275 |             files.push(path);
 276 |         } else {
 277 |             eprintln!("Warning: {} not found", input);
 278 |         }
 279 |     }
 280 | 
 281 |     files.sort();
 282 |     files
 283 | }
 284 | 
 285 | /// Read file contents. For JSONL, returns vec of text fields. For source files, returns vec of one.
 286 | fn read_file_contents(path: &PathBuf) -> Vec<Vec<u8>> {
 287 |     if path.extension().map_or(false, |e| e == "jsonl" || e == "json") {
 288 |         // JSONL: each line is {"text": "..."} — extract text field
 289 |         let file = match fs::File::open(path) {
 290 |             Ok(f) => f,
 291 |             Err(e) => {
 292 |                 eprintln!("  Error opening {}: {}", path.display(), e);
 293 |                 return vec![];
 294 |             }
 295 |         };
 296 |         let reader = io::BufReader::with_capacity(16 * 1024 * 1024, file);
 297 |         let mut docs = Vec::new();
 298 |         for line in reader.lines() {
 299 |             let line = match line {
 300 |                 Ok(l) => l,
 301 |                 Err(_) => continue,
 302 |             };
 303 |             if line.is_empty() {
 304 |                 continue;
 305 |             }
 306 |             // Fast JSON text extraction: look for "text" field
 307 |             if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&line) {
 308 |                 if let Some(text) = val.get("text").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
 309 |                     docs.push(text.as_bytes().to_vec());
 310 |                 } else if let Some(text) = val.get("content").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
 311 |                     docs.push(text.as_bytes().to_vec());
 312 |                 }
 313 |             }
 314 |         }
 315 |         docs
 316 |     } else {
 317 |         // Regular file: read entire contents
 318 |         match fs::read(path) {
 319 |             Ok(contents) => vec![contents],
 320 |             Err(e) => {
 321 |                 eprintln!("  Error reading {}: {}", path.display(), e);
 322 |                 vec![]
 323 |             }
 324 |         }
 325 |     }
 326 | }
 327 | 
 328 | // ─── Formatting helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 329 | 
 330 | fn format_num(n: u64) -> String {
 331 |     let s = n.to_string();
 332 |     let mut result = String::with_capacity(s.len() + s.len() / 3);
 333 |     for (i, c) in s.chars().rev().enumerate() {
 334 |         if i > 0 && i % 3 == 0 {
 335 |             result.push(',');
 336 |         }
 337 |         result.push(c);
 338 |     }
 339 |     result.chars().rev().collect()
 340 | }
 341 | 
 342 | fn status_label(count: u64) -> &'static str {
 343 |     match count {
 344 |         0 => "NOT FOUND",
 345 |         1..=9 => "RARE",
 346 |         10..=99 => "LOW",
 347 |         100..=999 => "MODERATE",
 348 |         1000..=9999 => "HIGH",
 349 |         _ => "VERY HIGH",
 350 |     }
 351 | }
 352 | 
 353 | // ─── Reporting ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 354 | 
 355 | fn print_category_report(
 356 |     name: &str,
 357 |     tokens: &[&str],
 358 |     mode: MatchMode,
 359 |     result: &GlobalResult,
 360 |     top: Option<usize>,
 361 | ) {
 362 |     // Collect token results
 363 |     let mut token_results: Vec<(&str, u64, u64)> = tokens
 364 |         .iter()
 365 |         .map(|&token| {
 366 |             let (count, doc_count) = match mode {
 367 |                 MatchMode::Ident => (
 368 |                     *result.ident_counts.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 369 |                     *result.doc_presence.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 370 |                 ),
 371 |                 MatchMode::Dollar => (
 372 |                     *result.dollar_counts.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 373 |                     *result.dollar_doc_presence.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 374 |                 ),
 375 |                 MatchMode::SqlString => (
 376 |                     *result.sql_keyword_counts.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 377 |                     *result.sql_doc_presence.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 378 |                 ),
 379 |             };
 380 |             (token, count, doc_count)
 381 |         })
 382 |         .collect();
 383 | 
 384 |     token_results.sort_by(|a, b| b.1.cmp(&a.1));
 385 |     if let Some(n) = top {
 386 |         token_results.truncate(n);
 387 |     }
 388 | 
 389 |     let found = token_results.iter().filter(|r| r.1 > 0).count();
 390 |     let total = tokens.len();
 391 |     let total_hits: u64 = token_results.iter().map(|r| r.1).sum();
 392 |     let coverage = if total > 0 {
 393 |         found as f64 / total as f64 * 100.0
 394 |     } else {
 395 |         0.0
 396 |     };
 397 | 
 398 |     println!("\n{}", "═".repeat(78));
 399 |     println!(
 400 |         "  {}  ({}/{} found, {:.0}% coverage)",
 401 |         name.to_uppercase().replace('_', " "),
 402 |         found,
 403 |         total,
 404 |         coverage,
 405 |     );
 406 |     println!("  Total occurrences: {}", format_num(total_hits));
 407 |     println!("{}", "═".repeat(78));
 408 | 
 409 |     println!(
 410 |         "  {:<45} {:>10} {:>8} {:>7}  {}",
 411 |         "Token", "Count", "Docs", "Doc%", "Status"
 412 |     );
 413 |     println!(
 414 |         "  {} {} {} {}  {}",
 415 |         "─".repeat(45),
 416 |         "─".repeat(10),
 417 |         "─".repeat(8),
 418 |         "─".repeat(7),
 419 |         "─".repeat(12),
 420 |     );
 421 | 
 422 |     let num_docs = result.num_docs;
 423 |     for (token, count, doc_count) in &token_results {
 424 |         let doc_pct = if num_docs > 0 {
 425 |             *doc_count as f64 / num_docs as f64 * 100.0
 426 |         } else {
 427 |             0.0
 428 |         };
 429 |         println!(
 430 |             "  {:<45} {:>10} {:>8} {:>6.1}%  {}",
 431 |             token,
 432 |             format_num(*count),
 433 |             format_num(*doc_count),
 434 |             doc_pct,
 435 |             status_label(*count),
 436 |         );
 437 |     }
 438 | }
 439 | 
 440 | fn print_morpheme_report(result: &GlobalResult, top: Option<usize>) {
 441 |     println!("\n{}", "═".repeat(78));
 442 |     println!("  MORPHEME STEM ANALYSIS");
 443 |     println!("{}", "═".repeat(78));
 444 | 
 445 |     for cat in MORPHEMES {
 446 |         let mut stems: Vec<(&str, u64)> = cat
 447 |             .stems
 448 |             .iter()
 449 |             .map(|&s| (s, *result.morpheme_counts.get(s).unwrap_or(&0)))
 450 |             .collect();
 451 |         stems.sort_by(|a, b| b.1.cmp(&a.1));
 452 |         if let Some(n) = top {
 453 |             stems.truncate(n);
 454 |         }
 455 | 
 456 |         let found = stems.iter().filter(|s| s.1 > 0).count();
 457 |         let total = cat.stems.len();
 458 |         let total_hits: u64 = stems.iter().map(|s| s.1).sum();
 459 | 
 460 |         println!("\n{}", "─".repeat(78));
 461 |         println!(
 462 |             "  {}  ({}/{} found, {:.0}% coverage)",
 463 |             cat.name.to_uppercase(),
 464 |             found,
 465 |             total,
 466 |             if total > 0 { found as f64 / total as f64 * 100.0 } else { 0.0 },
 467 |         );
 468 |         println!("  Total occurrences: {}", format_num(total_hits));
 469 | 
 470 |         println!(
 471 |             "  {:<30} {:>12}  {}",
 472 |             "Stem", "Count", "Status"
 473 |         );
 474 |         for (stem, count) in &stems {
 475 |             println!(
 476 |                 "  {:<30} {:>12}  {}",
 477 |                 stem,
 478 |                 format_num(*count),
 479 |                 status_label(*count),
 480 |             );
 481 |         }
 482 |     }
 483 | }
 484 | 
 485 | fn print_keyword_report(result: &GlobalResult, top: Option<usize>) {
 486 |     println!("\n{}", "═".repeat(78));
 487 |     println!("  C/C++ KEYWORD FREQUENCY");
 488 |     println!("{}", "═".repeat(78));
 489 | 
 490 |     let mut kws: Vec<(&str, u64)> = CPP_KEYWORDS
 491 |         .iter()
 492 |         .map(|&kw| (kw, *result.ident_counts.get(kw).unwrap_or(&0)))
 493 |         .collect();
 494 |     kws.sort_by(|a, b| b.1.cmp(&a.1));
 495 |     kws.dedup_by(|a, b| a.0 == b.0);
 496 |     if let Some(n) = top {
 497 |         kws.truncate(n);
 498 |     }
 499 | 
 500 |     let total: u64 = kws.iter().map(|k| k.1).sum();
 501 |     println!("  Total keyword occurrences: {}", format_num(total));
 502 |     println!(
 503 |         "  {:<25} {:>12} {:>8}  {}",
 504 |         "Keyword", "Count", "Doc%", "Status"
 505 |     );
 506 |     println!(
 507 |         "  {} {} {}  {}",
 508 |         "─".repeat(25),
 509 |         "─".repeat(12),
 510 |         "─".repeat(8),
 511 |         "─".repeat(12),
 512 |     );
 513 |     for (kw, count) in &kws {
 514 |         let doc_count = *result.doc_presence.get(*kw).unwrap_or(&0);
 515 |         let doc_pct = if result.num_docs > 0 {
 516 |             doc_count as f64 / result.num_docs as f64 * 100.0
 517 |         } else {
 518 |             0.0
 519 |         };
 520 |         println!(
 521 |             "  {:<25} {:>12} {:>7.1}%  {}",
 522 |             kw,
 523 |             format_num(*count),
 524 |             doc_pct,
 525 |             status_label(*count),
 526 |         );
 527 |     }
 528 | }
 529 | 
 530 | fn print_namespace_report(result: &GlobalResult, top: Option<usize>) {
 531 |     println!("\n{}", "═".repeat(78));
 532 |     println!("  NAMESPACE USAGE");
 533 |     println!("{}", "═".repeat(78));
 534 | 
 535 |     let mut ns: Vec<(&String, &u64)> = result.namespace_counts.iter().collect();
 536 |     ns.sort_by(|a, b| b.1.cmp(a.1));
 537 |     if let Some(n) = top {
 538 |         ns.truncate(n);
 539 |     }
 540 | 
 541 |     let total: u64 = ns.iter().map(|n| *n.1).sum();
 542 |     println!("  Total namespace-qualified refs: {}", format_num(total));
 543 |     println!(
 544 |         "  {:<30} {:>12}",
 545 |         "Namespace", "References"
 546 |     );
 547 |     println!("  {} {}", "─".repeat(30), "─".repeat(12));
 548 |     for (name, count) in &ns {
 549 |         println!("  {:<30} {:>12}", name, format_num(**count));
 550 |     }
 551 | }
 552 | 
 553 | fn print_style_report(result: &GlobalResult) {
 554 |     println!("\n{}", "═".repeat(78));
 555 |     println!("  IDENTIFIER NAMING STYLE DISTRIBUTION");
 556 |     println!("{}", "═".repeat(78));
 557 | 
 558 |     let s = &result.style_counts;
 559 |     let total = s.snake_case + s.camel_case + s.pascal_case + s.screaming_case + s.mixed_other;
 560 |     if total == 0 {
 561 |         println!("  No identifiers analyzed.");
 562 |         return;
 563 |     }
 564 | 
 565 |     let pct = |n: u64| n as f64 / total as f64 * 100.0;
 566 |     println!("  Total identifiers (len>2): {}", format_num(total));
 567 |     println!(
 568 |         "  {:<25} {:>12} {:>8}",
 569 |         "Style", "Count", "%"
 570 |     );
 571 |     println!("  {} {} {}", "─".repeat(25), "─".repeat(12), "─".repeat(8));
 572 |     println!("  {:<25} {:>12} {:>7.1}%", "snake_case", format_num(s.snake_case), pct(s.snake_case));
 573 |     println!("  {:<25} {:>12} {:>7.1}%", "camelCase", format_num(s.camel_case), pct(s.camel_case));
 574 |     println!("  {:<25} {:>12} {:>7.1}%", "PascalCase", format_num(s.pascal_case), pct(s.pascal_case));
 575 |     println!("  {:<25} {:>12} {:>7.1}%", "SCREAMING_CASE", format_num(s.screaming_case), pct(s.screaming_case));
 576 |     println!("  {:<25} {:>12} {:>7.1}%", "other/mixed", format_num(s.mixed_other), pct(s.mixed_other));
 577 | 
 578 |     // Mean identifier length
 579 |     if result.total_idents > 0 {
 580 |         let mean_len = result.total_ident_len as f64 / result.total_idents as f64;
 581 |         println!("\n  Mean identifier length: {:.1} chars", mean_len);
 582 |         println!("  Mean unique identifiers per doc: {:.0}",
 583 |             result.total_unique_idents as f64 / result.num_docs.max(1) as f64);
 584 |     }
 585 | }
 586 | 
 587 | fn print_content_report(result: &GlobalResult) {
 588 |     let s = &result.content_stats;
 589 |     println!("\n{}", "═".repeat(78));
 590 |     println!("  CONTENT ANALYSIS (Whitespace / Comments / Unicode)");
 591 |     println!("{}", "═".repeat(78));
 592 | 
 593 |     let pct = |n: u64, total: u64| {
 594 |         if total > 0 { n as f64 / total as f64 * 100.0 } else { 0.0 }
 595 |     };
 596 | 
 597 |     println!("\n  --- Byte Distribution ---");
 598 |     println!("  Total bytes:          {:>15}", format_num(s.total_bytes));
 599 |     println!("  Comment bytes:        {:>15} ({:.1}%)", format_num(s.comment_bytes), pct(s.comment_bytes, s.total_bytes));
 600 |     println!("  String literal bytes: {:>15} ({:.1}%)", format_num(s.string_bytes), pct(s.string_bytes, s.total_bytes));
 601 |     println!("  Code bytes (approx):  {:>15} ({:.1}%)",
 602 |         format_num(s.total_bytes.saturating_sub(s.comment_bytes).saturating_sub(s.string_bytes)),
 603 |         pct(s.total_bytes.saturating_sub(s.comment_bytes).saturating_sub(s.string_bytes), s.total_bytes));
 604 | 
 605 |     println!("\n  --- Lines ---");
 606 |     println!("  Total lines:          {:>15}", format_num(s.total_lines));
 607 |     println!("  Blank lines:          {:>15} ({:.1}%)", format_num(s.blank_lines), pct(s.blank_lines, s.total_lines));
 608 | 
 609 |     println!("\n  --- Comments ---");
 610 |     println!("  Line comments (//):   {:>15}", format_num(s.line_comments));
 611 |     println!("  Block comments (/*):  {:>15}", format_num(s.block_comments));
 612 |     println!("  Total comments:       {:>15}", format_num(s.line_comments + s.block_comments));
 613 | 
 614 |     println!("\n  --- Whitespace Patterns (BPE-relevant) ---");
 615 |     println!("  Single spaces:        {:>15}", format_num(s.single_spaces));
 616 |     println!("  Multi-space runs:     {:>15} (2+ consecutive spaces)", format_num(s.multi_spaces));
 617 |     println!("  Single tabs:          {:>15}", format_num(s.single_tabs));
 618 |     println!("  Multi-tab runs:       {:>15} (2+ consecutive tabs)", format_num(s.multi_tabs));
 619 |     println!("  Mixed indent lines:   {:>15} (spaces + tabs on same line)", format_num(s.mixed_indent));
 620 | 
 621 |     println!("\n  --- Indentation Style ---");
 622 |     println!("  2-space indent:       {:>15}", format_num(s.indent_2));
 623 |     println!("  4-space indent:       {:>15}", format_num(s.indent_4));
 624 |     println!("  8-space indent:       {:>15}", format_num(s.indent_8));
 625 |     println!("  Tab indent:           {:>15}", format_num(s.tab_indent));
 626 |     let total_indent = s.indent_2 + s.indent_4 + s.indent_8 + s.tab_indent;
 627 |     if total_indent > 0 {
 628 |         println!("  Dominant style:       {} ({:.0}%)",
 629 |             if s.indent_4 >= s.indent_2 && s.indent_4 >= s.tab_indent { "4-space" }
 630 |             else if s.indent_2 >= s.tab_indent { "2-space" }
 631 |             else { "tab" },
 632 |             pct(s.indent_4.max(s.indent_2).max(s.tab_indent), total_indent));
 633 |     }
 634 | 
 635 |     println!("\n  --- Unicode / Non-English Content ---");
 636 |     println!("  Non-ASCII bytes:      {:>15} ({:.3}% of total)", format_num(s.non_ascii_bytes), pct(s.non_ascii_bytes, s.total_bytes));
 637 |     println!("  UTF-8 multi-byte:     {:>15} characters", format_num(s.utf8_chars));
 638 |     println!("  Docs w/ non-ASCII comments: {:>9} ({:.1}% of docs)",
 639 |         format_num(result.docs_with_non_ascii_comments),
 640 |         pct(result.docs_with_non_ascii_comments, result.num_docs));
 641 | 
 642 |     if !result.non_english_samples.is_empty() {
 643 |         println!("\n  --- Non-English Comment Samples (first {}) ---", result.non_english_samples.len());
 644 |         for (i, sample) in result.non_english_samples.iter().enumerate().take(20) {
 645 |             let truncated = if sample.len() > 80 { &sample[..80] } else { sample.as_str() };
 646 |             println!("  {}. {}", i + 1, truncated);
 647 |         }
 648 |     }
 649 | 
 650 |     // BPE design recommendations
 651 |     println!("\n  --- BPE Design Implications ---");
 652 |     let space_ratio = if s.single_spaces + s.multi_spaces > 0 {
 653 |         s.multi_spaces as f64 / (s.single_spaces + s.multi_spaces) as f64 * 100.0
 654 |     } else { 0.0 };
 655 |     println!("  Multi-space ratio:    {:.1}% (of all space occurrences)", space_ratio);
 656 |     println!("  -> {} multi-space tokens in BPE vocab",
 657 |         if space_ratio < 5.0 { "Skip" } else if space_ratio < 20.0 { "Add few" } else { "Add several" });
 658 |     println!("  -> {} comment separator tokens",
 659 |         if s.comment_bytes as f64 / s.total_bytes as f64 > 0.15 { "Important — comments are >15% of corpus" }
 660 |         else { "Lower priority — comments are <15% of corpus" });
 661 |     println!("  -> Non-ASCII in comments: {}",
 662 |         if pct(result.docs_with_non_ascii_comments, result.num_docs) > 5.0 {
 663 |             "Significant — consider unicode-to-ASCII normalization"
 664 |         } else if pct(result.docs_with_non_ascii_comments, result.num_docs) > 1.0 {
 665 |             "Moderate — some non-English comments exist"
 666 |         } else {
 667 |             "Minimal — corpus is predominantly ASCII/English"
 668 |         });
 669 | }
 670 | 
 671 | fn print_summary(result: &GlobalResult) {
 672 |     println!("\n{}", "━".repeat(78));
 673 |     println!("  OVERALL SUMMARY  ({} documents analyzed)", format_num(result.num_docs));
 674 |     println!("{}", "━".repeat(78));
 675 | 
 676 |     println!(
 677 |         "\n  {:<30} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>12}",
 678 |         "Category", "Proposed", "Found", "Coverage", "Total Hits"
 679 |     );
 680 |     println!(
 681 |         "  {} {} {} {} {}",
 682 |         "─".repeat(30),
 683 |         "─".repeat(8),
 684 |         "─".repeat(8),
 685 |         "─".repeat(8),
 686 |         "─".repeat(12),
 687 |     );
 688 | 
 689 |     let mut grand_proposed = 0u64;
 690 |     let mut grand_found = 0u64;
 691 |     let mut grand_hits = 0u64;
 692 | 
 693 |     for cat in CATEGORIES {
 694 |         let total = cat.tokens.len();
 695 |         let mut found = 0;
 696 |         let mut hits = 0u64;
 697 |         for &token in cat.tokens {
 698 |             let count = match cat.mode {
 699 |                 MatchMode::Ident => *result.ident_counts.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 700 |                 MatchMode::Dollar => *result.dollar_counts.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 701 |                 MatchMode::SqlString => *result.sql_keyword_counts.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 702 |             };
 703 |             if count > 0 {
 704 |                 found += 1;
 705 |             }
 706 |             hits += count;
 707 |         }
 708 | 
 709 |         let coverage = if total > 0 {
 710 |             found as f64 / total as f64 * 100.0
 711 |         } else {
 712 |             0.0
 713 |         };
 714 | 
 715 |         grand_proposed += total as u64;
 716 |         grand_found += found as u64;
 717 |         grand_hits += hits;
 718 | 
 719 |         println!(
 720 |             "  {:<30} {:>8} {:>8} {:>7.0}% {:>12}",
 721 |             cat.name, total, found, coverage, format_num(hits),
 722 |         );
 723 |     }
 724 | 
 725 |     println!(
 726 |         "  {} {} {} {} {}",
 727 |         "─".repeat(30),
 728 |         "─".repeat(8),
 729 |         "─".repeat(8),
 730 |         "─".repeat(8),
 731 |         "─".repeat(12),
 732 |     );
 733 |     let grand_coverage = if grand_proposed > 0 {
 734 |         grand_found as f64 / grand_proposed as f64 * 100.0
 735 |     } else {
 736 |         0.0
 737 |     };
 738 |     println!(
 739 |         "  {:<30} {:>8} {:>8} {:>7.0}% {:>12}",
 740 |         "TOTAL",
 741 |         grand_proposed,
 742 |         grand_found,
 743 |         grand_coverage,
 744 |         format_num(grand_hits),
 745 |     );
 746 | }
 747 | 
 748 | // ─── JSON Output ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 749 | 
 750 | #[derive(Serialize)]
 751 | struct JsonOutput {
 752 |     num_docs: u64,
 753 |     total_idents: u64,
 754 |     mean_ident_len: f64,
 755 |     elapsed_s: f64,
 756 |     vocab: HashMap<String, Vec<JsonToken>>,
 757 |     morphemes: HashMap<String, Vec<JsonStem>>,
 758 |     style: JsonStyle,
 759 |     top_namespaces: Vec<JsonNamespace>,
 760 |     top_keywords: Vec<JsonKeyword>,
 761 | }
 762 | 
 763 | #[derive(Serialize)]
 764 | struct JsonToken {
 765 |     token: String,
 766 |     count: u64,
 767 |     doc_count: u64,
 768 |     doc_pct: f64,
 769 |     status: String,
 770 | }
 771 | 
 772 | #[derive(Serialize)]
 773 | struct JsonStem {
 774 |     stem: String,
 775 |     count: u64,
 776 | }
 777 | 
 778 | #[derive(Serialize)]
 779 | struct JsonStyle {
 780 |     snake_case: u64,
 781 |     camel_case: u64,
 782 |     pascal_case: u64,
 783 |     screaming_case: u64,
 784 |     mixed_other: u64,
 785 | }
 786 | 
 787 | #[derive(Serialize)]
 788 | struct JsonNamespace {
 789 |     name: String,
 790 |     count: u64,
 791 | }
 792 | 
 793 | #[derive(Serialize)]
 794 | struct JsonKeyword {
 795 |     keyword: String,
 796 |     count: u64,
 797 | }
 798 | 
 799 | fn write_json(result: &GlobalResult, elapsed: f64, path: &str) {
 800 |     let mut vocab: HashMap<String, Vec<JsonToken>> = HashMap::new();
 801 | 
 802 |     for cat in CATEGORIES {
 803 |         let mut tokens: Vec<JsonToken> = cat
 804 |             .tokens
 805 |             .iter()
 806 |             .map(|&token| {
 807 |                 let (count, doc_count) = match cat.mode {
 808 |                     MatchMode::Ident => (
 809 |                         *result.ident_counts.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 810 |                         *result.doc_presence.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 811 |                     ),
 812 |                     MatchMode::Dollar => (
 813 |                         *result.dollar_counts.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 814 |                         *result.dollar_doc_presence.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 815 |                     ),
 816 |                     MatchMode::SqlString => (
 817 |                         *result.sql_keyword_counts.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 818 |                         *result.sql_doc_presence.get(token).unwrap_or(&0),
 819 |                     ),
 820 |                 };
 821 |                 let doc_pct = if result.num_docs > 0 {
 822 |                     doc_count as f64 / result.num_docs as f64 * 100.0
 823 |                 } else {
 824 |                     0.0
 825 |                 };
 826 |                 JsonToken {
 827 |                     token: token.to_owned(),
 828 |                     count,
 829 |                     doc_count,
 830 |                     doc_pct,
 831 |                     status: status_label(count).to_owned(),
 832 |                 }
 833 |             })
 834 |             .collect();
 835 |         tokens.sort_by(|a, b| b.count.cmp(&a.count));
 836 |         vocab.insert(cat.name.to_owned(), tokens);
 837 |     }
 838 | 
 839 |     let mut morphemes_json: HashMap<String, Vec<JsonStem>> = HashMap::new();
 840 |     for cat in MORPHEMES {
 841 |         let mut stems: Vec<JsonStem> = cat
 842 |             .stems
 843 |             .iter()
 844 |             .map(|&s| JsonStem {
 845 |                 stem: s.to_owned(),
 846 |                 count: *result.morpheme_counts.get(s).unwrap_or(&0),
 847 |             })
 848 |             .collect();
 849 |         stems.sort_by(|a, b| b.count.cmp(&a.count));
 850 |         morphemes_json.insert(cat.name.to_owned(), stems);
 851 |     }
 852 | 
 853 |     let mut ns: Vec<JsonNamespace> = result
 854 |         .namespace_counts
 855 |         .iter()
 856 |         .map(|(k, v)| JsonNamespace {
 857 |             name: k.clone(),
 858 |             count: *v,
 859 |         })
 860 |         .collect();
 861 |     ns.sort_by(|a, b| b.count.cmp(&a.count));
 862 |     ns.truncate(100);
 863 | 
 864 |     let mut kws: Vec<JsonKeyword> = CPP_KEYWORDS
 865 |         .iter()
 866 |         .map(|&kw| JsonKeyword {
 867 |             keyword: kw.to_owned(),
 868 |             count: *result.ident_counts.get(kw).unwrap_or(&0),
 869 |         })
 870 |         .collect();
 871 |     kws.sort_by(|a, b| b.count.cmp(&a.count));
 872 |     kws.dedup_by(|a, b| a.keyword == b.keyword);
 873 | 
 874 |     let mean_len = if result.total_idents > 0 {
 875 |         result.total_ident_len as f64 / result.total_idents as f64
 876 |     } else {
 877 |         0.0
 878 |     };
 879 | 
 880 |     let output = JsonOutput {
 881 |         num_docs: result.num_docs,
 882 |         total_idents: result.total_idents,
 883 |         mean_ident_len: mean_len,
 884 |         elapsed_s: elapsed,
 885 |         vocab,
 886 |         morphemes: morphemes_json,
 887 |         style: JsonStyle {
 888 |             snake_case: result.style_counts.snake_case,
 889 |             camel_case: result.style_counts.camel_case,
 890 |             pascal_case: result.style_counts.pascal_case,
 891 |             screaming_case: result.style_counts.screaming_case,
 892 |             mixed_other: result.style_counts.mixed_other,
 893 |         },
 894 |         top_namespaces: ns,
 895 |         top_keywords: kws,
 896 |     };
 897 | 
 898 |     let file = fs::File::create(path).expect("Failed to create output file");
 899 |     let writer = BufWriter::with_capacity(64 * 1024 * 1024, file);
 900 |     serde_json::to_writer_pretty(writer, &output).expect("Failed to write JSON");
 901 |     eprintln!("JSON results saved to {}", path);
 902 | }
 903 | 
 904 | // ─── Main ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 905 | 
 906 | fn main() {
 907 |     let args = Args::parse();
 908 | 
 909 |     // Configure rayon thread pool
 910 |     if args.threads > 0 {
 911 |         rayon::ThreadPoolBuilder::new()
 912 |             .num_threads(args.threads)
 913 |             .build_global()
 914 |             .expect("Failed to set thread count");
 915 |     }
 916 |     let num_threads = rayon::current_num_threads();
 917 | 
 918 |     let do_morphemes = args.morphemes || args.all;
 919 |     let do_keywords = args.keywords || args.all;
 920 |     let do_namespaces = args.namespaces || args.all;
 921 |     let do_styles = args.styles || args.all;
 922 |     let do_content = args.content || args.all;
 923 | 
 924 |     // Collect files
 925 |     eprintln!("Collecting files from {} input path(s)...", args.inputs.len());
 926 |     let t_collect = Instant::now();
 927 |     let mut files = collect_files(&args.inputs, &args.extensions);
 928 |     if let Some(max) = args.max_files {
 929 |         files.truncate(max);
 930 |     }
 931 |     eprintln!(
 932 |         "Found {} files in {:.1}s",
 933 |         format_num(files.len() as u64),
 934 |         t_collect.elapsed().as_secs_f64(),
 935 |     );
 936 | 
 937 |     if files.is_empty() {
 938 |         eprintln!("Error: No files found");
 939 |         std::process::exit(1);
 940 |     }
 941 | 
 942 |     // Calculate total size
 943 |     let total_bytes: u64 = files.iter().filter_map(|f| fs::metadata(f).ok()).map(|m| m.len()).sum();
 944 |     eprintln!(
 945 |         "Total size: {:.2} GB, using {} threads",
 946 |         total_bytes as f64 / 1e9,
 947 |         num_threads,
 948 |     );
 949 | 
 950 |     // Process files in parallel using rayon
 951 |     let t0 = Instant::now();
 952 |     let files_done = AtomicU64::new(0);
 953 |     let bytes_done = AtomicU64::new(0);
 954 |     let total_files = files.len() as u64;
 955 | 
 956 |     // Process in parallel batches to reduce merge overhead
 957 |     let batch_size = std::cmp::max(100, files.len() / (num_threads * 4));
 958 |     let batches: Vec<&[PathBuf]> = files.chunks(batch_size).collect();
 959 | 
 960 |     eprintln!("Processing {} batches of ~{} files...", batches.len(), batch_size);
 961 | 
 962 |     let batch_results: Vec<GlobalResult> = batches
 963 |         .par_iter()
 964 |         .map(|batch| {
 965 |             let mut local = GlobalResult::new();
 966 |             for path in *batch {
 967 |                 let docs = read_file_contents(path);
 968 |                 let file_size = fs::metadata(path).map(|m| m.len()).unwrap_or(0);
 969 | 
 970 |                 for doc_bytes in &docs {
 971 |                     let doc_result = analyze_document(doc_bytes, do_morphemes, do_content);
 972 |                     local.merge_doc(doc_result);
 973 |                 }
 974 | 
 975 |                 let done = files_done.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed) + 1;
 976 |                 bytes_done.fetch_add(file_size, Ordering::Relaxed);
 977 | 
 978 |                 // Progress every 10K files
 979 |                 if done % 10_000 == 0 || done == total_files {
 980 |                     let elapsed = t0.elapsed().as_secs_f64();
 981 |                     let gb_done = bytes_done.load(Ordering::Relaxed) as f64 / 1e9;
 982 |                     let rate = done as f64 / elapsed;
 983 |                     eprintln!(
 984 |                         "  [{}/{}] {:.1} GB, {:.0} files/sec, {:.1}s",
 985 |                         format_num(done),
 986 |                         format_num(total_files),
 987 |                         gb_done,
 988 |                         rate,
 989 |                         elapsed,
 990 |                     );
 991 |                 }
 992 |             }
 993 |             local
 994 |         })
 995 |         .collect();
 996 | 
 997 |     // Merge all batch results
 998 |     eprintln!("Merging {} batch results...", batch_results.len());
 999 |     let mut result = GlobalResult::new();
1000 |     for batch in batch_results {
1001 |         merge_globals(&mut result, batch);
1002 |     }
1003 | 
1004 |     if do_keywords {
1005 |         result.compute_keyword_counts();
1006 |     }
1007 | 
1008 |     let elapsed = t0.elapsed().as_secs_f64();
1009 |     let throughput_gb = total_bytes as f64 / 1e9 / elapsed;
1010 |     eprintln!(
1011 |         "\nCompleted: {} docs from {} files in {:.1}s ({:.1} GB/s, {:.0} files/sec)",
1012 |         format_num(result.num_docs),
1013 |         format_num(total_files),
1014 |         elapsed,
1015 |         throughput_gb,
1016 |         total_files as f64 / elapsed,
1017 |     );
1018 | 
1019 |     // Write JSON output if requested
1020 |     if let Some(ref output_path) = args.output {
1021 |         write_json(&result, elapsed, output_path);
1022 |     }
1023 | 
1024 |     // Print reports
1025 |     let cat_filter = args.category.as_deref().map(|c| c.to_lowercase());
1026 | 
1027 |     for cat in CATEGORIES {
1028 |         if let Some(ref filter) = cat_filter {
1029 |             if !cat.name.to_lowercase().contains(filter.as_str()) {
1030 |                 continue;
1031 |             }
1032 |         }
1033 |         print_category_report(cat.name, cat.tokens, cat.mode, &result, args.top);
1034 |     }
1035 | 
1036 |     if do_morphemes {
1037 |         print_morpheme_report(&result, args.top);
1038 |     }
1039 | 
1040 |     if do_keywords {
1041 |         print_keyword_report(&result, args.top);
1042 |     }
1043 | 
1044 |     if do_namespaces {
1045 |         print_namespace_report(&result, args.top);
1046 |     }
1047 | 
1048 |     if do_styles {
1049 |         print_style_report(&result);
1050 |     }
1051 | 
1052 |     if do_content {
1053 |         print_content_report(&result);
1054 |     }
1055 | 
1056 |     // Always print summary
1057 |     print_summary(&result);
1058 | 
1059 |     // Flush stdout
1060 |     let _ = io::stdout().lock().flush();
1061 | }
```

```rust
  1 | /// Token pattern definitions for vocabulary frequency analysis.
  2 | ///
  3 | /// Each category has a match mode and list of tokens:
  4 | /// - Ident: match as C++ identifiers (word boundary)
  5 | /// - Dollar: match $-prefixed operators
  6 | /// - SqlString: match uppercase keywords inside string literals
  7 | 
  8 | #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]

 10 |     Ident,

 12 |     SqlString,
```

```rust
 1 | use std::sync::OnceLock;
 2 | use tokenizers::Tokenizer;
 3 | 
 4 | static TOKENIZER: OnceLock<Option<Tokenizer>> = OnceLock::new();
 5 | 
 6 | pub fn get_token_count(text: &str) -> usize {
 7 |     let tok = TOKENIZER.get_or_init(|| Tokenizer::from_pretrained("gpt2", None).ok());
 8 | 
 9 |     if let Some(tokenizer) = tok {
10 |         if let Ok(encoded) = tokenizer.encode(text, false) {
11 |             return encoded.get_ids().len();
12 |         }
13 |     }
14 |     // Fallback heuristic
15 |     std::cmp::max(1, text.len() / 4)
16 | }
```

```python
  1 | """Cppmega tokenizer wrapper with M0.1 contract checks.
  2 | 
  3 | The loader intentionally refuses artifacts that do not exactly match the
  4 | documented M0.1 tokenizer contract.  This avoids silently training against a
  5 | nearby nanochat tokenizer with different reserved IDs.
  6 | """
  7 | 
  8 | from __future__ import annotations
  9 | 
 10 | import json
 11 | import re
 12 | from collections.abc import Iterable, Sequence
 13 | from pathlib import Path
 14 | from typing import Any
 15 | 
 16 | EXPECTED_VOCAB_SIZE = 65_536
 17 | EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS: dict[str, int] = {
 18 |     "<BOS>": 2,
 19 |     "<EOS>": 3,
 20 |     "<FIM_PREFIX>": 4,
 21 |     "<FIM_MIDDLE>": 5,
 22 |     "<FIM_SUFFIX>": 6,
 23 |     "<CODE_START>": 7,
 24 |     "<CODE_END>": 8,
 25 |     "<THINK_START>": 9,
 26 |     "<THINK_END>": 10,
 27 |     "<QUERY_TOOL>": 11,
 28 |     "<TOOL_RESULT>": 19,
 29 |     "<FIM_INSTRUCTION>": 45,
 30 |     "<SPACE>": 46,
 31 |     "<NL>": 47,
 32 | }
 33 | 
 34 | 
 35 | # Whitespace normalization: collapse runs to a single sentinel token so that
 36 | # BPE-split identifiers (e.g., "sum" -> "s","u","m") decode without spurious
 37 | # inter-token spaces. Encode normalizes; decode replaces sentinels back.
 38 | _WS_RUN_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+")
 39 | _NL_RUN_RE = re.compile(r"[\r\n]+")
 40 | 
 41 | 
 42 | class TokenizerContractError(ValueError):
 43 |     """Raised when a tokenizer artifact does not satisfy M0.1."""
 44 | 
 45 | 
 46 | class CppMegaTokenizer:
 47 |     """Thin wrapper around ``tokenizers.Tokenizer`` with stable cppmega APIs."""
 48 | 
 49 |     def __init__(self, tokenizer: Any, *, path: Path):
 50 |         self._tokenizer = tokenizer
 51 |         self.path = path
 52 |         self._vocab: dict[str, int] = dict(tokenizer.get_vocab())
 53 |         self._id_to_token = {token_id: token for token, token_id in self._vocab.items()}
 54 |         self._space_token_id = EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<SPACE>"]
 55 |         self._nl_token_id = EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<NL>"]
 56 | 
 57 |     @property
 58 |     def vocab_size(self) -> int:
 59 |         return int(self._tokenizer.get_vocab_size(with_added_tokens=True))
 60 | 
 61 |     @property
 62 |     def bos_token_id(self) -> int:
 63 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<BOS>"]
 64 | 
 65 |     @property
 66 |     def eos_token_id(self) -> int:
 67 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<EOS>"]
 68 | 
 69 |     @property
 70 |     def fim_prefix_id(self) -> int:
 71 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<FIM_PREFIX>"]
 72 | 
 73 |     @property
 74 |     def fim_middle_id(self) -> int:
 75 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<FIM_MIDDLE>"]
 76 | 
 77 |     @property
 78 |     def fim_suffix_id(self) -> int:
 79 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<FIM_SUFFIX>"]
 80 | 
 81 |     @property
 82 |     def code_start_id(self) -> int:
 83 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<CODE_START>"]
 84 | 
 85 |     @property
 86 |     def code_end_id(self) -> int:
 87 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<CODE_END>"]
 88 | 
 89 |     @property
 90 |     def think_start_id(self) -> int:
 91 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<THINK_START>"]
 92 | 
 93 |     @property
 94 |     def think_end_id(self) -> int:
 95 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<THINK_END>"]
 96 | 
 97 |     @property
 98 |     def query_tool_id(self) -> int:
 99 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<QUERY_TOOL>"]
100 | 
101 |     @property
102 |     def tool_result_id(self) -> int:
103 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<TOOL_RESULT>"]
104 | 
105 |     @property
106 |     def fim_instruction_id(self) -> int:
107 |         return EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS["<FIM_INSTRUCTION>"]
108 | 
109 |     @property
110 |     def space_token_id(self) -> int:
111 |         return self._space_token_id
112 | 
113 |     @property
114 |     def nl_token_id(self) -> int:
115 |         return self._nl_token_id
116 | 
117 |     def get_vocab_size(self) -> int:
118 |         return self.vocab_size
119 | 
120 |     @staticmethod
121 |     def _normalize_whitespace(text: str) -> str:
122 |         """Collapse whitespace runs to ``<SPACE>``/``<NL>`` sentinel tokens."""
123 |         text = _NL_RUN_RE.sub("<NL>", text)
124 |         text = _WS_RUN_RE.sub("<SPACE>", text)
125 |         return text
126 | 
127 |     def encode(
128 |         self,
129 |         text: str | Sequence[str],
130 |         *,
131 |         prepend: int | str | None = None,
132 |         append: int | str | None = None,
133 |     ) -> list[int] | list[list[int]]:
134 |         prepend_id = self._resolve_optional_token(prepend)
135 |         append_id = self._resolve_optional_token(append)
136 | 
137 |         if isinstance(text, str):
138 |             normalized = self._normalize_whitespace(text)
139 |             ids = list(self._tokenizer.encode(normalized).ids)
140 |             return self._with_optional_tokens(ids, prepend_id, append_id)
141 |         if isinstance(text, Sequence):
142 |             normalized_batch = [self._normalize_whitespace(t) for t in text]
143 |             rows = [
144 |                 list(encoded.ids)
145 |                 for encoded in self._tokenizer.encode_batch(normalized_batch)
146 |             ]
147 |             return [
148 |                 self._with_optional_tokens(row, prepend_id, append_id) for row in rows
149 |             ]
150 |         raise TypeError(f"text must be str or sequence[str], got {type(text).__name__}")
151 | 
152 |     def encode_batch(self, texts: Sequence[str]) -> list[list[int]]:
153 |         normalized = [self._normalize_whitespace(t) for t in texts]
154 |         return [list(encoded.ids) for encoded in self._tokenizer.encode_batch(normalized)]
155 | 
156 |     def decode(self, ids: Iterable[int]) -> str:
157 |         """Decode IDs by simple concat then replacing ``<SPACE>``/``<NL>`` sentinels.
158 | 
159 |         The encoder substitutes whitespace runs with ``<SPACE>``/``<NL>`` tokens so
160 |         that BPE-split identifiers decode without spurious inter-token spaces.
161 |         Byte-exact for any input whose internal whitespace runs are at most a
162 |         single space or single newline (longer runs are collapsed by the
163 |         normalizer). Matches the CUDA-side ``nanochat.cpp_tokenizer`` decode.
164 |         """
165 |         parts = [self._id_to_token.get(int(i), "") for i in ids]
166 |         s = "".join(parts)
167 |         return s.replace("<SPACE>", " ").replace("<NL>", "\n")
168 | 
169 |     def token_for_id(self, token_id: int) -> str | None:
170 |         return self._id_to_token.get(token_id)
171 | 
172 |     def id_for_token(self, token: str) -> int | None:
173 |         return self._vocab.get(token)
174 | 
175 |     def __call__(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
176 |         return self.encode(*args, **kwargs)
177 | 
178 |     def _resolve_optional_token(self, token: int | str | None) -> int | None:
179 |         if token is None:
180 |             return None
181 |         if isinstance(token, int) and not isinstance(token, bool):
182 |             return token
183 |         if isinstance(token, str):
184 |             token_id = self.id_for_token(token)
185 |             if token_id is None:
186 |                 raise TokenizerContractError(f"unknown special token {token!r}")
187 |             return token_id
188 |         raise TypeError(f"token must be int or str, got {type(token).__name__}")
189 | 
190 |     @staticmethod
191 |     def _with_optional_tokens(
192 |         ids: list[int], prepend_id: int | None, append_id: int | None
193 |     ) -> list[int]:
194 |         if prepend_id is not None:
195 |             ids.insert(0, prepend_id)
196 |         if append_id is not None:
197 |             ids.append(append_id)
198 |         return ids
199 | 
200 | 
201 | def load_cppmega_tokenizer(path: str | Path) -> CppMegaTokenizer:
202 |     """Load a tokenizer only if it satisfies the M0.1 contract."""
203 | 
204 |     tokenizer_path = _resolve_tokenizer_path(path)
205 |     payload = _load_tokenizer_json(tokenizer_path)
206 |     vocab = _extract_vocab(payload, tokenizer_path)
207 |     _validate_vocab_contract(vocab, tokenizer_path)
208 | 
209 |     try:
210 |         from tokenizers import Tokenizer  # pyright: ignore[reportMissingImports]
211 |     except ModuleNotFoundError as exc:
212 |         raise TokenizerContractError(
213 |             "tokenizers package is required to load cppmega tokenizer artifacts"
214 |         ) from exc
215 | 
216 |     return CppMegaTokenizer(Tokenizer.from_file(str(tokenizer_path)), path=tokenizer_path)
217 | 
218 | 
219 | def _resolve_tokenizer_path(path: str | Path) -> Path:
220 |     candidate = Path(path)
221 |     if candidate.is_dir():
222 |         candidate = candidate / "tokenizer.json"
223 |     if not candidate.is_file():
224 |         raise FileNotFoundError(f"tokenizer artifact not found: {candidate}")
225 |     return candidate
226 | 
227 | 
228 | def _load_tokenizer_json(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
229 |     try:
230 |         payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
231 |     except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
232 |         raise TokenizerContractError(f"{path}: invalid tokenizer JSON") from exc
233 |     if not isinstance(payload, dict):
234 |         raise TokenizerContractError(f"{path}: tokenizer JSON must be an object")
235 |     return payload
236 | 
237 | 
238 | def _extract_vocab(payload: dict[str, Any], path: Path) -> dict[str, int]:
239 |     model = payload.get("model")
240 |     if not isinstance(model, dict):
241 |         raise TokenizerContractError(f"{path}: missing tokenizer model")
242 |     raw_vocab = model.get("vocab")
243 |     if not isinstance(raw_vocab, dict):
244 |         raise TokenizerContractError(f"{path}: missing tokenizer model vocab")
245 | 
246 |     vocab: dict[str, int] = {}
247 |     seen_ids: set[int] = set()
248 |     for token, token_id in raw_vocab.items():
249 |         if not isinstance(token, str) or not isinstance(token_id, int):
250 |             raise TokenizerContractError(f"{path}: vocab entries must be str->int")
251 |         if token_id in seen_ids:
252 |             raise TokenizerContractError(f"{path}: duplicate vocab id {token_id}")
253 |         seen_ids.add(token_id)
254 |         vocab[token] = token_id
255 |     return vocab
256 | 
257 | 
258 | def _validate_vocab_contract(vocab: dict[str, int], path: Path) -> None:
259 |     if len(vocab) != EXPECTED_VOCAB_SIZE:
260 |         raise TokenizerContractError(
261 |             f"{path}: expected vocab size {EXPECTED_VOCAB_SIZE}, got {len(vocab)}"
262 |         )
263 | 
264 |     id_to_token = {token_id: token for token, token_id in vocab.items()}
265 |     if len(id_to_token) != len(vocab):
266 |         raise TokenizerContractError(f"{path}: vocab ids must be unique")
267 | 
268 |     for token, expected_id in EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS.items():
269 |         actual_id = vocab.get(token)
270 |         if actual_id != expected_id:
271 |             occupant = id_to_token.get(expected_id)
272 |             raise TokenizerContractError(
273 |                 f"{path}: token {token!r} must use id {expected_id}, "
274 |                 f"got {actual_id}; id {expected_id} maps to {occupant!r}"
275 |             )
276 |         actual_token = id_to_token.get(expected_id)
277 |         if actual_token != token:
278 |             raise TokenizerContractError(
279 |                 f"{path}: id {expected_id} must map to {token!r}, got {actual_token!r}"
280 |             )
281 | 
282 | 
283 | __all__ = [
284 |     "CppMegaTokenizer",
285 |     "EXPECTED_SPECIAL_TOKENS",
286 |     "EXPECTED_VOCAB_SIZE",
287 |     "TokenizerContractError",
288 |     "load_cppmega_tokenizer",
289 | ]
```

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/cppmega.mlx/cppmega_mlx/tokenizer/fingerprint.py
