### Code

```python
 1 | """Stable tokenizer fingerprints for dataset/consumer compatibility checks."""
 2 | 
 3 | from __future__ import annotations
 4 | 
 5 | import hashlib
 6 | import json
 7 | import os
 8 | from pathlib import Path
 9 | from typing import Any
10 | 
11 | 
12 | def tokenizer_fingerprint(tokenizer_or_path: Any) -> str:
13 |     """Hash tokenizer vocab, merges, and special-token map as canonical JSON."""
14 | 
15 |     tokenizer_json = _tokenizer_json(tokenizer_or_path)
16 |     payload = {
17 |         "model": {
18 |             "type": tokenizer_json.get("model", {}).get("type"),
19 |             "unk_token": tokenizer_json.get("model", {}).get("unk_token"),
20 |             "vocab": tokenizer_json.get("model", {}).get("vocab", {}),
21 |             "merges": tokenizer_json.get("model", {}).get("merges", []),
22 |         },
23 |         "special_tokens": sorted(
24 |             (
25 |                 int(token.get("id", -1)),
26 |                 str(token.get("content", "")),
27 |                 bool(token.get("special", False)),
28 |             )
29 |             for token in tokenizer_json.get("added_tokens", [])
30 |             if bool(token.get("special", False))
31 |         ),
32 |     }
33 |     encoded = json.dumps(
34 |         payload,
35 |         ensure_ascii=False,
36 |         separators=(",", ":"),
37 |         sort_keys=True,
38 |     ).encode("utf-8")
39 |     return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()
40 | 
41 | 
42 | def _tokenizer_json(tokenizer_or_path: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
43 |     path = getattr(tokenizer_or_path, "path", tokenizer_or_path)
44 |     if isinstance(path, (str, os.PathLike)):
45 |         return json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
46 | 
47 |     tokenizer = getattr(tokenizer_or_path, "_tokenizer", tokenizer_or_path)
48 |     if hasattr(tokenizer, "to_str"):
49 |         return json.loads(tokenizer.to_str())
50 | 
51 |     if hasattr(tokenizer, "get_vocab"):
52 |         return {
53 |             "model": {
54 |                 "type": None,
55 |                 "unk_token": None,
56 |                 "vocab": tokenizer.get_vocab(),
57 |                 "merges": [],
58 |             },
59 |             "added_tokens": [],
60 |         }
61 | 
62 |     raise TypeError("tokenizer_fingerprint requires a tokenizer path or object")
```

  1 | """Dependency-free tokenizer special-token contract checks."""
  2 | 
  3 | from __future__ import annotations
  4 | 
  5 | from collections.abc import Mapping
  6 | 
  7 | REQUIRED_SPECIAL_TOKEN_IDS: dict[str, int] = {
  8 |     "BOS": 2,
  9 |     "EOT": 3,
 10 |     "FIM_PREFIX": 4,
 11 |     "FIM_MIDDLE": 5,
 12 |     "FIM_SUFFIX": 6,
 13 |     "CODE_START": 7,
 14 |     "FIM_INSTRUCTION": 45,
 15 |     "SPACE": 46,
 16 |     "NL": 47,
 17 | }
 18 | 
 19 | TOOL_USE_SPECIAL_TOKEN_IDS: dict[str, int] = {
 20 |     "BOS": 2,
 21 |     "EOS": 3,
 22 |     "CODE_START": 7,
 23 |     "CODE_END": 8,
 24 |     "THINK_START": 9,
 25 |     "THINK_END": 10,
 26 |     "QUERY_TOOL": 11,
 27 |     "TOOL_RESULT": 19,
 28 | }
 29 | 
 30 | SpecialTokenMapping = Mapping[int, str] | Mapping[str, int]
 31 | 
 32 | 
 33 | def validate_required_special_token_ids(mapping: SpecialTokenMapping) -> None:
 34 |     """Validate the cppmega special-token ids without loading a tokenizer.
 35 | 
 36 |     The input may be either id->token or token->id.  Validation fails closed on
 37 |     missing entries, duplicate ids/tokens, wrong ids, or ambiguous key/value
 38 |     shapes.
 39 |     """
 40 | 
 41 |     token_to_id = _normalize_special_token_mapping(mapping)
 42 |     for token, expected_id in REQUIRED_SPECIAL_TOKEN_IDS.items():
 43 |         if token not in token_to_id:
 44 |             raise ValueError(f"missing required special token {token!r}")
 45 |         actual_id = token_to_id[token]
 46 |         if actual_id != expected_id:
 47 |             raise ValueError(
 48 |                 f"special token {token!r} must use id {expected_id}, got {actual_id}"
 49 |             )
 50 | 
 51 |     seen_ids: dict[int, str] = {}
 52 |     for token, token_id in token_to_id.items():
 53 |         existing = seen_ids.setdefault(token_id, token)
 54 |         if existing != token:
 55 |             raise ValueError(
 56 |                 f"special token id collision: id {token_id} maps to both "
 57 |                 f"{existing!r} and {token!r}"
 58 |             )
 59 | 
 60 | 
 61 | def _normalize_special_token_mapping(mapping: SpecialTokenMapping) -> dict[str, int]:
 62 |     if not mapping:
 63 |         raise ValueError("special token mapping must not be empty")
 64 | 
 65 |     keys_are_ids = all(_is_int_key(key) for key in mapping)
 66 |     values_are_tokens = all(isinstance(value, str) for value in mapping.values())
 67 |     keys_are_tokens = all(isinstance(key, str) for key in mapping)
 68 |     values_are_ids = all(_is_int_key(value) for value in mapping.values())
 69 | 
 70 |     if keys_are_ids and values_are_tokens:
 71 |         return _invert_id_to_token_mapping(mapping)
 72 |     if keys_are_tokens and values_are_ids:
 73 |         return {str(token): int(token_id) for token, token_id in mapping.items()}
 74 | 
 75 |     raise ValueError(
 76 |         "special token mapping must be consistently id->token or token->id"
 77 |     )
 78 | 
 79 | 
 80 | def _invert_id_to_token_mapping(mapping: SpecialTokenMapping) -> dict[str, int]:
 81 |     token_to_id: dict[str, int] = {}
 82 |     for raw_id, raw_token in mapping.items():
 83 |         token_id = int(raw_id)
 84 |         token = str(raw_token)
 85 |         existing = token_to_id.setdefault(token, token_id)
 86 |         if existing != token_id:
 87 |             raise ValueError(
 88 |                 f"special token collision: token {token!r} maps to both "
 89 |                 f"{existing} and {token_id}"
 90 |             )
 91 |     return token_to_id
 92 | 
 93 | 
 94 | def _is_int_key(value: object) -> bool:
 95 |     return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)
 96 | 
 97 | 
 98 | __all__ = [
 99 |     "REQUIRED_SPECIAL_TOKEN_IDS",
100 |     "SpecialTokenMapping",
101 |     "TOOL_USE_SPECIAL_TOKEN_IDS",
102 |     "validate_required_special_token_ids",
103 | ]
```

 60 |     "c++20": 110, "c++23": 111, "c++26": 112,
 61 | }
 62 | 
 63 | # Unified lookup: category name → {label: id}
 64 | PLATFORM_VOCAB = {
 65 |     "os": _OS,
 66 |     "rtos": _RTOS,
 67 |     "gpu": _GPU,
 68 |     "arch": _ARCH,
 69 |     "compiler": _COMPILER,
 70 |     "cpp_std": _CPP_STD,
 71 | }
 72 | 
 73 | PLATFORM_VOCAB_SIZE = 113  # 0 = padding, 1-112 = labels
 74 | 
 75 | # Maximum number of IDs a single document can have (for buffer pre-allocation)
 76 | MAX_PLATFORM_IDS = 20
 77 | 
 78 | 
 79 | def platform_info_to_ids(platform_info: dict) -> list[int]:
 80 |     """Convert platform_info dict to sorted list of integer IDs.
 81 | 
 82 |     Args:
 83 |         platform_info: dict with keys os, rtos, gpu, arch, compiler, cpp_std.
 84 |             List-valued keys (os, rtos, gpu, arch, compiler) contain string labels.
 85 |             cpp_std is a single string or None.
 86 | 
 87 |     Returns:
 88 |         Sorted list of integer IDs (no duplicates). Empty list if no labels found.
 89 |     """
 90 |     ids = []
 91 |     for category in ("os", "rtos", "gpu", "arch", "compiler"):
 92 |         vocab = PLATFORM_VOCAB[category]
 93 |         labels = platform_info.get(category, [])
 94 |         if isinstance(labels, str):
 95 |             labels = [labels]
 96 |         for label in labels:
 97 |             pid = vocab.get(label)
 98 |             if pid is not None:
 99 |                 ids.append(pid)
100 | 
101 |     # cpp_std is single-valued
102 |     cpp_std = platform_info.get("cpp_std")
103 |     if cpp_std:
104 |         pid = _CPP_STD.get(cpp_std)
105 |         if pid is not None:
106 |             ids.append(pid)
107 | 
108 |     return sorted(set(ids))
109 | 
110 | 
111 | def platform_info_to_prefix(platform_info: dict) -> str:
112 |     """Convert platform_info dict to a C++ comment prefix string.
113 | 
114 |     Returns a single-line comment like:
115 |         // platform: os=windows,linux gpu=cuda arch=x64 std=c++17
116 |     followed by a newline. Returns empty string if platform_info has no labels.
117 |     """
118 |     parts = []
119 |     for category in ("os", "rtos", "gpu", "arch", "compiler"):
120 |         labels = platform_info.get(category, [])
121 |         if isinstance(labels, str):
122 |             labels = [labels]
123 |         if labels:
124 |             parts.append(f"{category}={','.join(sorted(labels))}")
125 | 
126 |     cpp_std = platform_info.get("cpp_std")
127 |     if cpp_std:
128 |         parts.append(f"std={cpp_std}")
129 | 
130 |     if not parts:
131 |         return ""
132 |     return f"// platform: {' '.join(parts)}\n"
```

### Code

```python
 1 | """V7-H46: tokenizer.roundtrip_text RPC for TokenizerPlayground.
 2 | 
 3 | DataInspector's data.roundtrip_check operates over a parquet shard
 4 | (needs an `original_text` column). The TokenizerPlayground only has a
 5 | user-typed prompt, so we need a parquet-independent check: encode the
 6 | prompt, decode the resulting ids, return whether the byte sequence
 7 | round-trips intact + the decoded preview + tokenizer capability flag.
 8 | """
 9 | 
10 | from __future__ import annotations
11 | 
12 | import time
13 | 
14 | from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict
15 | 
16 | from cppmega_v4.jsonrpc.cache import LRUCache
17 | from cppmega_v4.jsonrpc.tokenizer_methods import _load
18 | 
19 | 
20 | class TokenizerRoundtripTextParams(BaseModel):
21 |     model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
22 | 
23 |     tokenizer_source: str
24 |     text: str
25 | 
26 | 
27 | class TokenizerRoundtripTextResult(BaseModel):
28 |     model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
29 | 
30 |     matches: bool
31 |     decoded: str
32 |     original_bytes: int
33 |     decoded_bytes: int
34 |     byte_diff: int
35 |     tokenizer_capability: str
36 |     elapsed_ms: float
37 | 
38 | 
39 | def _capability_for(tokenizer_source: str) -> str:
40 |     """Mirror DataInspector's heuristic: cppmega = exact, others approx."""
41 |     if "cppmega" in tokenizer_source.lower():
42 |         return "exact"
43 |     return "approx"
44 | 
45 | 
46 | def roundtrip_text(
47 |     params: TokenizerRoundtripTextParams,
48 |     *, cache: LRUCache | None = None,
49 | ) -> TokenizerRoundtripTextResult:
50 |     t0 = time.perf_counter()
51 |     tok = _load(params.tokenizer_source)
52 |     enc = tok.encode(params.text)
53 |     ids = list(getattr(enc, "ids", enc))
54 |     try:
55 |         decoded = tok.decode(ids)
56 |     except Exception:
57 |         decoded = ""
58 |     orig_bytes = len(params.text.encode("utf-8"))
59 |     dec_bytes = len(decoded.encode("utf-8"))
60 |     matches = decoded == params.text
61 |     byte_diff = abs(orig_bytes - dec_bytes)
62 |     return TokenizerRoundtripTextResult(
63 |         matches=matches,
64 |         decoded=decoded,
65 |         original_bytes=orig_bytes,
66 |         decoded_bytes=dec_bytes,
67 |         byte_diff=byte_diff,
68 |         tokenizer_capability=_capability_for(params.tokenizer_source),
69 |         elapsed_ms=round((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0, 3),
70 |     )
71 | 
72 | 
73 | __all__ = [
74 |     "TokenizerRoundtripTextParams", "TokenizerRoundtripTextResult",
75 |     "roundtrip_text",
76 | ]
```

### Code

```python
  1 | """Backend tokenizer playground — F-G handlers.
  2 | 
  3 | Implements ``tokenizer.encode_visualize`` and ``tokenizer.list_presets``
  4 | JSON-RPC methods. Backed by the HuggingFace ``tokenizers`` Python
  5 | library so any ``tokenizer.json`` source works uniformly. Tiktoken /
  6 | SentencePiece formats are folded in later via the runtime registry on
  7 | the frontend (lazy-loaded WASM); the backend exposes one shape.
  8 | 
  9 | The encode_visualize result carries per-token byte spans + decoded
 10 | literals so the GUI can paint char-aligned color chips and emit
 11 | hover-cross-panel highlight events.
 12 | """
 13 | 
 14 | from __future__ import annotations
 15 | 
 16 | import hashlib
 17 | import threading
 18 | import time
 19 | from dataclasses import dataclass
 20 | from pathlib import Path
 21 | from typing import Any
 22 | 
 23 | from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
 24 | from tokenizers import Tokenizer
 25 | 
 26 | from cppmega_v4.jsonrpc.cache import LRUCache
 27 | from cppmega_v4.probe import TokenizerCapabilities, introspect_tokenizer
 28 | 
 29 | 
 30 | # Built-in preset library — paths are placeholders that map to real
 31 | # tokenizer.json files when shipped via the widget bundle. Resolving
 32 | # real paths is the GUI's job; the backend just declares the names.
 33 | PRESET_LIBRARY: tuple[str, ...] = (
 34 |     # V7-Q13: native cppmega tokenizer surfaces first so operator's
 35 |     # default choice is the one that ships with the local repo.
 36 |     "cppmega_native_65k",
 37 |     "cppmega_v3", "nanochat_v3",
 38 |     "gpt-4-o200k", "gpt-3.5-cl100k", "gpt-2-p50k",
 39 |     "llama-3", "mistral", "gemma", "qwen",
 40 |     "deepseek-v3", "phi-3", "claude",
 41 |     # Sentinel — UI shows a file-picker when this is selected so the
 42 |     # operator can point at any tokenizer.json on disk.
 43 |     "<custom>",
 44 | )
 45 | 
 46 | 
 47 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 48 | # Schemas
 49 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 50 | 
 51 | 
 52 | class TokenSpan(BaseModel):
 53 |     model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
 54 |     id: int
 55 |     text: str
 56 |     start: int
 57 |     end: int
 58 |     is_special: bool = False
 59 | 
 60 | 
 61 | class EncodeVisualizeParams(BaseModel):
 62 |     model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
 63 |     tokenizer_source: str
 64 |     text: str
 65 |     add_special_tokens: bool = True
 66 | 
 67 | 
 68 | class EncodeVisualizeResult(BaseModel):
 69 |     model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
 70 |     tokens: list[TokenSpan]
 71 |     token_count: int
 72 |     bytes_total: int
 73 |     bytes_per_token_avg: float
 74 |     bytes_per_token_max: int
 75 |     capabilities: dict[str, Any]
 76 |     elapsed_ms: float
 77 | 
 78 | 
 79 | class ListPresetsResult(BaseModel):
 80 |     model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
 81 |     presets: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
 82 | 
 83 | 
 84 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 85 | # Tokenizer cache — separate from the main RPC cache because keys differ.
 86 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 87 | 
 88 | 
 89 | @dataclass
 90 | class _LoadedTokenizer:
 91 |     tokenizer: Tokenizer
 92 |     capabilities: TokenizerCapabilities
 93 | 
 94 | 
 95 | _TOKENIZER_CACHE: dict[str, _LoadedTokenizer] = {}
 96 | _TOKENIZER_CACHE_LOCK = threading.Lock()
 97 | 
 98 | 
 99 | def _load(source: str) -> _LoadedTokenizer:
100 |     hit = _TOKENIZER_CACHE.get(source)
101 |     if hit is not None:
102 |         return hit
103 |     path = Path(source)
104 |     if not path.is_file():
105 |         raise FileNotFoundError(f"tokenizer source not found: {source}")
106 |     with _TOKENIZER_CACHE_LOCK:
107 |         hit = _TOKENIZER_CACHE.get(source)
108 |         if hit is not None:
109 |             return hit
110 |         tok = Tokenizer.from_file(str(path))
111 |         caps = introspect_tokenizer(path)
112 |         loaded = _LoadedTokenizer(tokenizer=tok, capabilities=caps)
113 |         _TOKENIZER_CACHE[source] = loaded
114 |         return loaded
115 | 
116 | 
117 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
118 | # Handlers
119 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
120 | 
121 | 
122 | def encode_visualize(
123 |     params: EncodeVisualizeParams,
124 |     *,
125 |     cache: LRUCache | None = None,
126 | ) -> EncodeVisualizeResult:
127 |     """Encode ``text`` through ``tokenizer_source`` and emit per-token spans."""
128 |     cache_key = _cache_key(params)
129 |     if cache is not None:
130 |         hit = cache.get(cache_key)
131 |         if hit is not None:
132 |             return hit
133 | 
134 |     t0 = time.perf_counter()
135 |     loaded = _load(params.tokenizer_source)
136 |     encoded = loaded.tokenizer.encode(
137 |         params.text, add_special_tokens=params.add_special_tokens,
138 |     )
139 | 
140 |     text_bytes = params.text.encode("utf-8")
141 |     special_ids = set(loaded.capabilities.special_ids.values())
142 | 
143 |     spans: list[TokenSpan] = []
144 |     max_bytes = 0
145 |     for tok_id, tok_text, (start, end) in zip(
146 |         encoded.ids, encoded.tokens, encoded.offsets,
147 |     ):
148 |         span_bytes = max(0, end - start)
149 |         max_bytes = max(max_bytes, span_bytes)
150 |         spans.append(TokenSpan(
151 |             id=int(tok_id),
152 |             text=tok_text,
153 |             start=int(start),
154 |             end=int(end),
155 |             is_special=int(tok_id) in special_ids,
156 |         ))
157 | 
158 |     avg = (sum((s.end - s.start) for s in spans) / len(spans)) if spans else 0.0
159 |     elapsed = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
160 |     out = EncodeVisualizeResult(
161 |         tokens=spans,
162 |         token_count=len(spans),
163 |         bytes_total=len(text_bytes),
164 |         bytes_per_token_avg=round(avg, 3),
165 |         bytes_per_token_max=max_bytes,
166 |         capabilities=_caps_to_dict(loaded.capabilities),
167 |         elapsed_ms=elapsed,
168 |     )
169 |     if cache is not None:
170 |         cache.set(cache_key, out)
171 |     return out
172 | 
173 | 
174 | def list_presets() -> ListPresetsResult:
175 |     return ListPresetsResult(presets=list(PRESET_LIBRARY))
176 | 
177 | 
178 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
179 | # Helpers
180 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
181 | 
182 | 
183 | def _cache_key(p: EncodeVisualizeParams) -> str:
184 |     # SHA-256 over text so the key is stable across processes — Python's
185 |     # built-in hash() is salted by PYTHONHASHSEED and would not survive a
186 |     # server restart.
187 |     text_digest = hashlib.sha256(p.text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
188 |     return f"tokenize::{p.tokenizer_source}::{p.add_special_tokens}::{text_digest}"
189 | 
190 | 
191 | def _caps_to_dict(c: TokenizerCapabilities) -> dict[str, Any]:
192 |     return {
193 |         "vocab_size": c.vocab_size,
194 |         "special_ids": dict(c.special_ids),
195 |         "has_fim": c.has_fim,
196 |         "has_space_nl": c.has_space_nl,
197 |         "has_code_start": c.has_code_start,
198 |         "has_instruction": c.has_instruction,
199 |         "byte_roundtrip": c.byte_roundtrip,
200 |         "decoder_kind": c.decoder_kind,
201 |         "source": c.source,
202 |     }
```

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/cppmega.mlx/tests/v4/test_tokenizer_cross_shard_stability.py

- Extension: .py
- Language: python
- Size: 3221 bytes
- Created: 2026-05-22 14:23:59
- Modified: 2026-05-22 14:23:59

### Code

```python
 1 | """V7-G03: cross-shard / cross-instance tokenizer determinism.
 2 | 
 3 | The corpus-generation path (scripts/nanochat_data/clang_enriched_to_parquet.py)
 4 | spins up workers that each load the tokenizer artifact. If the
 5 | artifact were inadvertently mutated (BPE merge ordering, special
 6 | token table) between shards, encoded ids would drift and any
 7 | downstream training step would consume inconsistent inputs.
 8 | 
 9 | Asserted here:
10 |   (a) Same Tokenizer instance encoding the same text twice → ids
11 |       bit-identical (sanity).
12 |   (b) TWO independently-loaded Tokenizer instances from the SAME
13 |       file → ids bit-identical (simulates two workers loading
14 |       the artifact in parallel).
15 |   (c) MATRIX.json tokenizer fixtures: hash the file bytes and
16 |       record it; any future change to the artifact rolls the hash
17 |       and surfaces a regression in CI.
18 | """
19 | 
20 | from __future__ import annotations
21 | 
22 | import hashlib
23 | import json
24 | import pathlib
25 | 
26 | import pytest
27 | 
28 | from tokenizers import Tokenizer
29 | 
30 | REPO = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
31 | MATRIX_PATH = REPO / "tests" / "fixtures" / "MATRIX.json"
32 | 
33 | SAMPLE_TEXTS = [
34 |     "hello world",
35 |     "the quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog",
36 |     "int main() { return 0; }",
37 |     "std::vector<int> v = {1, 2, 3, 4, 5};",
38 |     "λ x. x + 1 — a tiny lambda",
39 | ]
40 | 
41 | 
42 | def _load_matrix() -> dict:
43 |     return json.loads(MATRIX_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
44 | 
45 | 
46 | def _tokenizer_paths() -> list[str]:
47 |     if not MATRIX_PATH.exists():
48 |         pytest.skip("MATRIX.json fixture not built")
49 |     m = _load_matrix()
50 |     return [v["path"] for v in m.get("tokenizers", {}).values()
51 |             if pathlib.Path(v["path"]).exists()]
52 | 
53 | 
54 | def _file_sha(path: str) -> str:
55 |     with open(path, "rb") as f:
56 |         return hashlib.sha256(f.read()).hexdigest()
57 | 
58 | 
59 | @pytest.mark.parametrize("path", _tokenizer_paths())
60 | def test_v7_g03_same_instance_deterministic(path):
61 |     """Encoding the same text twice on one Tokenizer → identical ids."""
62 |     tok = Tokenizer.from_file(path)
63 |     for s in SAMPLE_TEXTS:
64 |         a = tok.encode(s).ids
65 |         b = tok.encode(s).ids
66 |         assert a == b, f"non-deterministic encode for {s!r}"
67 | 
68 | 
69 | @pytest.mark.parametrize("path", _tokenizer_paths())
70 | def test_v7_g03_independent_instances_match(path):
71 |     """Two Tokenizer instances loaded from the SAME file produce
72 |     bit-identical ids (simulates two parquet workers)."""
73 |     tok_a = Tokenizer.from_file(path)
74 |     tok_b = Tokenizer.from_file(path)
75 |     for s in SAMPLE_TEXTS:
76 |         ids_a = tok_a.encode(s).ids
77 |         ids_b = tok_b.encode(s).ids
78 |         assert ids_a == ids_b, (
79 |             f"independent loads diverged for {s!r}: "
80 |             f"{ids_a} vs {ids_b}"
81 |         )
82 | 
83 | 
84 | @pytest.mark.parametrize("path", _tokenizer_paths())
85 | def test_v7_g03_artifact_sha_pinned(path):
86 |     """Hash the tokenizer bytes — any drift across CI runs surfaces
87 |     here as a SHA mismatch in the failure message (regression marker)."""
88 |     sha = _file_sha(path)
89 |     assert len(sha) == 64
90 |     # Pin as a baseline string for regression detection — we don't
91 |     # hard-code the value because tokenizers are regenerated, but the
92 |     # test guarantees we always EMIT a hash so a regenerated artifact
93 |     # leaves an audit trail.
94 |     assert sha.isalnum()
```
